                "needs_review": result.get("needs_review"),
                "level_explanation": result.get("level_explanation") or {},
                "category_key": result.get("category_key") or run.category_key,
                "dimension_selection": result.get("dimension_selection"),
                "versions": result.get("versions") or {},
                "correction_context": _correction_context(item),
            }
        )
    if any(row["category_key"] != run.category_key for row in frozen_rows):
        raise ValueError("偏差样本与基准回归类目不一致")

    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-correction-input-v2",
        "baseline_run_id": run.id,
        "baseline_set_id": run.baseline_set_id,
        "baseline_set_fingerprint": run.baseline_set_fingerprint,
        "category_key": run.category_key,
        "strategy_bundle_id": run.strategy_bundle_id,
        "execution_snapshot": execution_snapshot,
        "correction_contract": (
            {
                "contract_version": _json_object(
                    run.correction_contract_json
                ).get("contract_version"),
                "contract_hash": run.correction_contract_hash,
                "category_key": run.category_key,
            }
            if getattr(run, "correction_contract_hash", None)
            else None
        ),
        "dimension_selection": dimension_selection,
        "run_metrics": _json_object(run.metrics_json),
        "items": frozen_rows,
    }


def _rank_counts(counter: dict[str, int]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [
        {"key": key, "count": count}
        for key, count in sorted(
            counter.items(), key=lambda pair: (-pair[1], pair[0])
        )
    ]


def deterministic_correction_report(
    input_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Analyze frozen deviations without a model call or publication side effect."""

    raw_items = input_snapshot.get("items")
    if not isinstance(raw_items, list) or not raw_items:
        raise ValueError("纠偏分析输入不包含样本")
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    direction_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    distance_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    pair_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    weak_dimension_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    quality_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    cap_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    sample_evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    samples_with_human_evidence = 0
    human_evidence_count = 0
    automatic_evidence_count = 0
    affected_layer_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for raw in raw_items:
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            raise ValueError("纠偏分析样本结构损坏")
        expected = raw.get("expected_level")
        predicted = raw.get("predicted_level")
        if expected not in LEVELS or predicted not in LEVELS or expected == predicted:
            raise ValueError("纠偏分析仅接受有效偏差样本")
        expected_index = LEVELS.index(expected)
        predicted_index = LEVELS.index(predicted)
        distance = abs(expected_index - predicted_index)
        direction = "under_rated" if predicted_index > expected_index else "over_rated"
        pair = f"{expected}→{predicted}"
        direction_counts[direction] = direction_counts.get(direction, 0) + 1
        distance_counts[str(distance)] = distance_counts.get(str(distance), 0) + 1
        pair_counts[pair] = pair_counts.get(pair, 0) + 1
        explanation = raw.get("level_explanation")
        explanation = explanation if isinstance(explanation, dict) else {}
        for dimension in explanation.get("weak_dimensions") or []:
            if isinstance(dimension, dict) and isinstance(dimension.get("key"), str):
                key = dimension["key"]
                weak_dimension_counts[key] = weak_dimension_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
        quality = explanation.get("image_quality")
        if isinstance(quality, dict) and isinstance(quality.get("severity"), str):
            key = quality["severity"]
            quality_counts[key] = quality_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
        for cap in explanation.get("caps") or []:
            if isinstance(cap, dict):
                reason = cap.get("reason") or cap.get("cap")
            else:
                reason = cap
            if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
                key = reason.strip()
                cap_counts[key] = cap_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
        context = raw.get("correction_context")
        context = context if isinstance(context, dict) else {}
        node_corrections = context.get("node_corrections")
        node_corrections = (
            [event for event in node_corrections if isinstance(event, dict)]
            if isinstance(node_corrections, list)
            else []
        )
        human_node_corrections = [
            event for event in node_corrections if event.get("source") == "human"
        ]
        human_reviews = context.get("human_reviews")
        human_reviews = (
            [review for review in human_reviews if isinstance(review, dict)]
            if isinstance(human_reviews, list)
            else []
        )
        frozen_human_count = context.get("human_evidence_count")
        sample_human_count = (
            frozen_human_count
            if isinstance(frozen_human_count, int)
            and not isinstance(frozen_human_count, bool)
            and frozen_human_count >= 0
            else len(human_node_corrections) + len(human_reviews)
        )
        frozen_automatic_count = context.get("automatic_evidence_count")
        sample_automatic_count = (
            frozen_automatic_count
            if isinstance(frozen_automatic_count, int)
            and not isinstance(frozen_automatic_count, bool)
            and frozen_automatic_count >= 0
            else sum(
                1
                for event in node_corrections
                if event.get("source") == "automatic"
            )
        )
        raw_layers = context.get("affected_layers")
        sample_layers = (
            [
                layer
                for layer in _CORRECTION_LAYER_ORDER
                if layer in raw_layers
            ]
            if isinstance(raw_layers, list)
            else []
        )
        if sample_human_count:
            samples_with_human_evidence += 1
        human_evidence_count += sample_human_count
        automatic_evidence_count += sample_automatic_count
        for layer in sample_layers:
            affected_layer_counts[layer] = (
                affected_layer_counts.get(layer, 0) + 1
            )
        sample_evidence.append(
            {
                "item_id": int(raw["item_id"]),
                "asset_id": raw.get("asset_id"),
                "evaluation_id": context.get("evaluation_id")
                or raw.get("evaluation_id"),
                "review_stage": context.get("review_stage"),
                "review_revision": context.get("review_revision"),
                "final_review_id": context.get("final_review_id"),
                "affected_layers": sample_layers,
                "human_evidence_count": sample_human_count,
                "human_node_corrections": human_node_corrections,
                "human_reviews": human_reviews,
                "manual_notes": context.get("manual_notes", []),
                "excluded_automatic_evidence_count": sample_automatic_count,
            }
        )
        rows.append(
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "expected_level": expected,
                "predicted_level": predicted,
            }
        )

    accuracy = compute_level_metrics(rows)
    average_distance = sum(
        int(distance) * count for distance, count in distance_counts.items()
    ) / len(raw_items)
    dimension_selection = input_snapshot.get("dimension_selection")
    dimension_selection = (
        dimension_selection if isinstance(dimension_selection, dict) else {}
    )
    top_direction = _rank_counts(direction_counts)[0]["key"]
    prompt_recommendations = [
        {
            "code": "inspect_level_anchors",
            "priority": "high" if direction_counts[top_direction] >= len(raw_items) * 0.6 else "medium",
            "message": (
                "模型更常给出低于真值质量的等级，优先检查负向证据和封顶措辞。"
                if top_direction == "under_rated"
                else "模型更常给出高于真值质量的等级，优先收紧高等级锚点和反例。"
            ),
            "supporting_samples": direction_counts[top_direction],
        }
    ]
    if cap_counts:
        prompt_recommendations.append(
            {
                "code": "inspect_cap_evidence",
                "priority": "medium",
                "message": "偏差样本集中出现等级限制，核对提示词证据与服务端封顶条件。",
                "supporting_samples": sum(cap_counts.values()),
            }
        )
    if dimension_selection.get("mode") == "none":
        dimension_recommendations = []
    elif weak_dimension_counts:
        top_dimensions = [
            item["key"] for item in _rank_counts(weak_dimension_counts)[:3]
        ]
        dimension_recommendations = [
            {
                "dimension_key": key,
                "priority": "high" if weak_dimension_counts[key] >= max(3, len(raw_items) // 2) else "medium",
                "message": "复核该维度的定义、权重和证据锚点。",
                "signals": {"weak_dimension": weak_dimension_counts[key]},
            }
            for key in top_dimensions
        ]
    else:
        dimension_recommendations = []

    affected_layers = [
        layer
        for layer in _CORRECTION_LAYER_ORDER
        if affected_layer_counts.get(layer, 0) > 0
    ]
    required_prompt_stage = (
        affected_layers[0]
        if affected_layers in (["A"], ["B"])
        else None
    )
    allowed_prompt_stages = (
        [required_prompt_stage]
        if required_prompt_stage is not None
        else ["A", "B"]
    )
    route_decision: dict[str, Any] = {
        "layers": affected_layers,
        "route_key": "+".join(affected_layers),
        "dependency_order": affected_layers,
        "reason_codes": [],
        "evidence_paths": [],
        "confidence": "high" if affected_layers else "low",
    }
    if affected_layers:
        try:
            from .automation_routing import route_correction_evidence

            route = route_correction_evidence(
                {
                    "node_corrections": [
                        node
                        for item in raw_items
                        for node in item.get("human_node_corrections", [])
                    ]
                }
            )
            route_decision = {
                "layers": list(route.layers),
                "route_key": route.route_key,
                "dependency_order": list(route.dependency_order),
                "reason_codes": list(route.reason_codes),
                "evidence_paths": list(route.evidence_paths),
                "confidence": route.confidence,
            }
        except ValueError:
            # Keep the existing report usable for older snapshots; the new
            # intake gate will reject the same malformed evidence before use.
            pass

    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-correction-report-v2",
        "status": "automatic_candidate_pipeline",
        "category_key": input_snapshot.get("category_key"),
        "baseline_run_id": input_snapshot.get("baseline_run_id"),
        "selection": {
            "policy": "explicit_completed_deviations",
            "count": len(raw_items),
            "item_ids": [int(item["item_id"]) for item in raw_items],
        },
        "accuracy_report": {
            "run_metrics": input_snapshot.get("run_metrics") or {},
            "selected_deviation_count": len(raw_items),
            "average_level_distance": round(average_distance, 3),
            "direction_counts": direction_counts,
            "confusion_pairs": [
                {"pair": pair, "count": count}
                for pair, count in sorted(pair_counts.items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]))
            ],
        },
        "attribution": {
            "dominant_direction": top_direction,
            "prompt_only": dimension_selection.get("mode") == "none",
            "dimension_signal_count": len(weak_dimension_counts),
        },
        "evidence_summary": {
            "selected_sample_count": len(raw_items),
            "samples_with_human_evidence": samples_with_human_evidence,
            "human_evidence_count": human_evidence_count,
            "automatic_evidence_count": automatic_evidence_count,
            "coverage_rate": round(
                samples_with_human_evidence / len(raw_items),
                4,
            ),
            "affected_layer_counts": affected_layer_counts,
            "affected_layers": affected_layers,
        },
        "sample_evidence": sample_evidence,
        "candidate_routing": {
            "policy": "human_evidence_only",
            "affected_layers": affected_layers,
            "allowed_prompt_stages": allowed_prompt_stages,
            "required_prompt_stage": required_prompt_stage,
        },
        "route_decision": route_decision,
        "prompt_suggestions": prompt_recommendations,
        "dimension_suggestions": dimension_recommendations,
        "confidence": "high" if len(raw_items) >= 30 else "medium" if len(raw_items) >= 10 else "low",
        "risks": (["差异样本少于 10，建议仅作优化候选。"] if len(raw_items) < 10 else []),
        "publication": {
            "allowed": False,
            "next_state": "automatic_candidate_regression",
            "message": "系统将自动生成候选并执行回归；仅最终启用或拒绝需要人工决策。",
        },
    }


def execute_correction_run(correction: BaselineCorrectionRun) -> None:
    """Persist deterministic analysis before automatic orchestration continues."""

    try:
        snapshot = json.loads(correction.input_snapshot_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ValueError("纠偏分析冻结输入损坏") from exc
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        raise ValueError("纠偏分析冻结输入损坏")
    correction.status = "processing"
    correction.stage = "analysis"
    correction.progress = 25
    correction.report_json = canonical_json(
        deterministic_correction_report(snapshot)
    )
    correction.stage = "candidate_generation"
    correction.blockers_json = "[]"
    correction.error_code = ""
    correction.error_message = ""
    correction.finished_at = None


def fail_correction_run(
    correction: BaselineCorrectionRun,
    *,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
) -> None:
    correction.status = "failed"
    correction.progress = 0
    correction.blockers_json = canonical_json(
        [
            {
                "code": error_code,
                "message": error_message[:500],
                "retryable": correction.attempt_count < 3,
            }
        ]
    )
    correction.error_code = error_code[:80]
    correction.error_message = error_message[:500]
    correction.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

===== FILE backend/app/baseline_release_drain.py =====
"""Audited admission-only release drain. Never interrupt paid provider work."""
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document
import json
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .models import AuditEvent, BaselineRegressionItem, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeAuditEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation
from .native_baseline_incident import _contract, _facts


def _locked_child(db, run_id, contract):
    from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    aggregate, key = _contract(db, **contract)
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id) if run else None
    if (run is None or run.source_type != 'baseline_regression_item' or item is None
            or item.run_id != aggregate.id or item.production_run_id != run.id
            or run.workflow_version_id != contract['expected_workflow_id']):
        raise ValueError('release drain child scope mismatch')
    runtime = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get('runtime_context', {})
    if (runtime.get('baseline_run_id') != aggregate.id
            or runtime.get('baseline_item_id') != item.id
            or runtime.get('real_execution_budget', {}).get('max_cost_micros') != contract['expected_cost_cap']):
        raise ValueError('release drain child frozen contract mismatch')
    return run, f'{key}:release-drain:{run.id}'


def _audit_watermark(db, run):
    return db.scalar(select(func.max(RuntimeAuditEvent.id)).where(
        RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_type == 'production_run', RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == run.run_key)) or 0


def drain_child(db, *, run_id, **contract):
    run, key = _locked_child(db, run_id, contract)
    prior = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key+':pause'))
    if prior:
        return 'paused' if run.status == 'paused' else 'already_handled'
    if run.status in {'succeeded', 'failed', 'canceled'}:
        return 'terminal'
    if run.status not in {'running', 'queued', 'retryable'}:
        return 'untouched'
    # Claim and reserve also lock the child run first. Recheck after that lock
    # to close the selection/claim race without revoking any existing lease.
    active = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id, ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(['running','leased'])).limit(1))
    reserved = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id, WorkflowModelInvocation.status == 'reserved').limit(1))
    if active or reserved:
        return 'in_flight'
    payload = {'run_id':run.id, 'original_status':run.status, 'facts':_facts(db, run),
               'runtime_audit_watermark': _audit_watermark(db, run)}
    from .batch_pacing_maintenance import record_release_pool_maintenance
    record_release_pool_maintenance(db, run_id=run.id,
        incident=key.split(':release-drain:')[0], paused=True, actor=contract['actor'])
    run.status = 'paused'
    append_audit_event(db, category='baseline_regression', action='release_admission_pause',
        subject_type='production_run', subject_id=run.id, actor=contract['actor'], payload=payload, event_key=key+':pause')
    db.flush()
    return 'paused'


def resume_child(db, *, run_id, **contract):
    run, key = _locked_child(db, run_id, contract)
    if db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key+':resume')):
        return 'already_resumed'
    prior = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key+':pause'))
    if prior is None:
        return 'untouched'
    payload = json.loads(prior.payload_json)
    watermark = _audit_watermark(db, run)
    if (run.status != 'paused' or _facts(db, run) != payload['facts']
            or watermark != payload['runtime_audit_watermark']):
        raise ValueError('release drain state changed; preserve operator decision')
    from .batch_pacing_maintenance import record_release_pool_maintenance
    record_release_pool_maintenance(db, run_id=run.id,
        incident=key.split(':release-drain:')[0], paused=False, actor=contract['actor'])
    run.status = payload['original_status']
    append_audit_event(db, category='baseline_regression', action='release_admission_resume',
        subject_type='production_run', subject_id=run.id, actor=contract['actor'], payload=payload, event_key=key+':resume')
    db.flush()
    return 'resumed'

===== FILE backend/app/baseline_rule_diagnostics.py =====
"""Aggregate per-rule hit coverage for one baseline regression run.

Tuning a scoring mechanism blind is the failure mode this module exists to
prevent.  A contract can declare dozens of deduction rules, raise their point
values, and lower the redline threshold -- and still change nothing, because the
model never reports the defects those rules key on.  Without per-rule coverage
an operator sees only "accuracy is still bad" and keeps adjusting numbers that
can never fire.

The report answers three questions directly:

* which declared rules were never hit across the whole run (dead rules),
* how many items ended up with no rule hits at all (so their score is whatever
  calling B volunteered, untouched by the rule layer),
* where the resulting scores landed relative to the contract's level scale.

Everything is derived from already-persisted snapshots; nothing is recomputed
and no model call is made.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

from .level_scale import LevelScaleError, resolve_level_scale

RULE_DIAGNOSTICS_SCHEMA = "baseline-rule-diagnostics-v1"


def _schema_definitions(track: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    for group_key in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
        group = track.get(group_key)
        if not isinstance(group, Mapping):
            continue
        definition = group.get("schema_definition")
        if isinstance(definition, Mapping):
            yield definition


def declared_rules(subcategory_dimensions: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Flatten every deduction/bonus rule the frozen contract declares.

    Rules are keyed by ``(dimension_key, rule_id)`` because the same ``rule_id``
    may legitimately appear under several dimensions; a track that repeats a
    dimension key is folded into one entry so the report counts each rule once.
    """
    seen: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for track_key, track in sorted((subcategory_dimensions or {}).items()):
        if not isinstance(track, Mapping):
            continue
        for definition in _schema_definitions(track):
            for dimension in definition.get("dimensions") or []:
                if not isinstance(dimension, Mapping):
                    continue
                dimension_key = str(dimension.get("key") or "")
                if not dimension_key:
                    continue
                for kind, points_field in (
                    ("deduction", "deduction"),
                    ("bonus", "bonus"),
                ):
                    for rule in dimension.get(f"{kind}_rules") or []:
                        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping):
                            continue
                        rule_id = str(rule.get("rule_id") or "")
                        if not rule_id:
                            continue
                        key = (dimension_key, rule_id)
                        if key in seen:
                            seen[key].setdefault("tracks", [])
                            if track_key not in seen[key]["tracks"]:
                                seen[key]["tracks"].append(track_key)
                            continue
                        seen[key] = {
                            "dimension_key": dimension_key,
                            "dimension_label": str(dimension.get("label") or ""),
                            "rule_id": rule_id,
                            "kind": kind,
                            "description": str(rule.get("description") or ""),
                            "points": rule.get(points_field),
                            "tracks": [track_key],
                        }
    return [seen[key] for key in sorted(seen)]


def _hit_rule_ids(dimension_output: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    def ids(field: str) -> set[str]:
        out: set[str] = set()
        for entry in dimension_output.get(field) or []:
            if isinstance(entry, Mapping):
                rule_id = entry.get("rule_id")
                if rule_id:
                    out.add(str(rule_id))
            elif entry:
                out.add(str(entry))
        return out

    return ids("hit_rules"), ids("hit_bonus_rules")


def _level_for_score(contract: Mapping[str, Any], score: Any) -> str | None:
    """Map a score with the same level table the aggregator uses at runtime.

    ``resolve_level_scale`` honours whichever of ``level_scale`` /
    ``level_thresholds`` the frozen contract carries, so a mechanism frozen on
    legacy thresholds is no longer diagnosed against a different table.
    """
    if not isinstance(score, (int, float)) or isinstance(score, bool) or not contract:
        return None
    try:
        resolved = resolve_level_scale(dict(contract))
    except (LevelScaleError, TypeError, ValueError):
        # 非规范的 level_scale（缺版本号或未列全 L1-L5）按启用档尽力映射。
        return _lenient_level_for_score(contract.get("level_scale") or {}, score)
    for entry in resolved["thresholds"]:
        if score >= entry["min_score"]:
            return str(entry["level"])
    return None


def _lenient_level_for_score(level_scale: Mapping[str, Any], score: float) -> str | None:
    best: tuple[int, str] | None = None
    for level in (level_scale or {}).get("levels") or []:
        if not isinstance(level, Mapping) or level.get("enabled") is False:
            continue
        minimum = level.get("min_score")
        if not isinstance(minimum, (int, float)) or isinstance(minimum, bool):
            continue
        if score >= minimum and (best is None or minimum > best[0]):
            best = (int(minimum), str(level.get("level") or ""))
    return best[1] if best else None


def build_rule_diagnostics(
    *,
    frozen_bundle: Mapping[str, Any],
    item_snapshots: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Summarise rule coverage for one run from its frozen contract and items."""
    contract = frozen_bundle.get("contract")
    contract = contract if isinstance(contract, Mapping) else {}
    subcategory_dimensions = frozen_bundle.get("subcategory_dimensions")
    subcategory_dimensions = (
        subcategory_dimensions if isinstance(subcategory_dimensions, Mapping) else {}
    )
    rules = declared_rules(subcategory_dimensions)
    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {
        (rule["dimension_key"], rule["rule_id"]): 0 for rule in rules
    }
    undeclared: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}

    scored_items = 0
    items_without_hits = 0
    items_with_caps = 0
    level_distribution: dict[str, int] = {}
    score_buckets: dict[str, int] = {}
    unpenalised_scores: list[float] = []

    for snapshot in item_snapshots:
        if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
            continue
        scoring = snapshot.get("scoring")
        scoring = scoring if isinstance(scoring, Mapping) else {}
        output = scoring.get("dimension_deduction_output")
        output = output if isinstance(output, Mapping) else {}
        dimensions = output.get("dimensions")
        dimensions = dimensions if isinstance(dimensions, Mapping) else {}

        predicted = snapshot.get("predicted_level")
        if predicted:
            level_distribution[str(predicted)] = (
                level_distribution.get(str(predicted), 0) + 1
            )
        score = snapshot.get("authoritative_score")
        if isinstance(score, (int, float)) and not isinstance(score, bool):
            bucket = f"{int(score) // 10 * 10}-{int(score) // 10 * 10 + 9}"
            score_buckets[bucket] = score_buckets.get(bucket, 0) + 1
        if snapshot.get("cap_reasons"):
            items_with_caps += 1

        if not dimensions:
            continue
        scored_items += 1
        item_hits = 0
        for dimension_key, dimension_output in dimensions.items():
            if not isinstance(dimension_output, Mapping):
                continue
            deduction_hits, bonus_hits = _hit_rule_ids(dimension_output)
            for rule_id in deduction_hits | bonus_hits:
                key = (str(dimension_key), rule_id)
                item_hits += 1
                if key in counts:
                    counts[key] += 1
                else:
                    undeclared[key] = undeclared.get(key, 0) + 1
        if item_hits == 0:
            items_without_hits += 1
            # Score an item keeps when no rule fires -- i.e. whatever calling B
            # volunteered, passed through untouched by the rule layer.
            if isinstance(score, (int, float)) and not isinstance(score, bool):
                unpenalised_scores.append(score)

    rule_rows = [
        {**rule, "hit_count": counts[(rule["dimension_key"], rule["rule_id"])]}
        for rule in rules
    ]
    never_hit = [row for row in rule_rows if row["hit_count"] == 0]
    # Median, not mean: one outlier score should not move the figure an operator
    # uses to reason about where unpenalised items land on the level scale.
    unpenalised_score: float | None = None
    if unpenalised_scores:
        ordered = sorted(unpenalised_scores)
        middle = len(ordered) // 2
        unpenalised_score = (
            ordered[middle]
            if len(ordered) % 2
            else (ordered[middle - 1] + ordered[middle]) / 2
        )

    return {
        "schema_version": RULE_DIAGNOSTICS_SCHEMA,
        "candidate_revision_id": frozen_bundle.get("candidate_revision_id"),
        "config_revision": frozen_bundle.get("config_revision"),
        "rules": rule_rows,
        "never_hit_rule_count": len(never_hit),
        "declared_rule_count": len(rule_rows),
        "undeclared_hits": [
            {"dimension_key": key[0], "rule_id": key[1], "hit_count": value}
            for key, value in sorted(undeclared.items())
        ],
        "scored_item_count": scored_items,
        "items_without_rule_hits": items_without_hits,
        "items_with_score_caps": items_with_caps,
        "level_distribution": level_distribution,
        "score_buckets": dict(sorted(score_buckets.items())),
        "level_scale": contract.get("level_scale") or {},
        "redline_policy": {
            key: value
            for key, value in (contract.get("redline_policy") or {}).items()
            if key != "rules"
        },
        "redline_rule_count": len(
            (contract.get("redline_policy") or {}).get("rules") or []
        ),
        "rule_layer_inert": bool(scored_items) and items_without_hits == scored_items,
        "unpenalised_level": _level_for_score(contract, unpenalised_score),
    }


def diagnostics_from_run(run: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the report straight off a persisted regression run."""
    execution = json.loads(getattr(run, "execution_snapshot_json", None) or "{}")
    bundle = execution.get("v3_authoritative_bundle")
    bundle = bundle if isinstance(bundle, Mapping) else {}
    snapshots = [
        json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json or "{}")
        for item in getattr(run, "items", []) or []
    ]
    return build_rule_diagnostics(frozen_bundle=bundle, item_snapshots=snapshots)

===== FILE backend/app/baseline_summary_projection.py =====
"""Read only the frozen JSON fragments needed by history summaries.

These are projections, never a second source of truth or a cached settlement.
Nested evidence objects retain their JSON values. SQLite represents extracted
scalar booleans as integers; consumers reject either as non-object evidence.
Other dialects retain the original column and the existing Python validation.
"""
from sqlalchemy import case, func


def summary_json_projection(db, column, *fields: str):
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name not in {"mysql", "sqlite"}:
        return column
    valid = case((func.json_valid(column) == 1, column), else_="{}")
    arguments = []
    for field in fields:
        arguments.extend((field, func.json_extract(valid, f"$.{field}")))
    return func.json_object(*arguments)

class BaselineTruthError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EffectiveBaselineTruth:
    source: str
    truth_kind: str
    expected_level: str | None
    expected_result: dict[str, Any]
    truth_contract_key: str
    workflow_input: dict[str, Any]
    revision: int
    revision_id: int | None
    canonical_hash: str


def _json_object(value: str | None, *, code: str, message: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        decoded = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise BaselineTruthError(code, message, status_code=422) from exc
    if not isinstance(decoded, dict):
        raise BaselineTruthError(code, message, status_code=422)
    return decoded


def _canonical_object(value: Mapping[str, Any], *, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        decoded = json.loads(canonical_json(dict(value)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            code,
            "人工真值只允许可序列化的 JSON 对象",
            status_code=422,
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(decoded, dict):
        raise BaselineTruthError(code, "人工真值必须是 JSON 对象", status_code=422)
    return decoded


def _normalize_truth(
    *,
    expected_level: str | None,
    expected_result: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str, str | None, dict[str, Any]]:
    normalized = _canonical_object(
        expected_result,
        code="baseline_truth_result_invalid",
    )
    if expected_level is not None:
        level = str(expected_level).strip()
        if level not in _LEVELS:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_level_invalid",
                "等级真值只能是 L1-L5",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if normalized.get("level") != level:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_level_mismatch",
                "等级真值与结构化结果中的 level 不一致",
                status_code=422,
            )
        return "level", level, normalized
    if not normalized:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_result_empty",
            "结构化人工真值不能为空对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return "structured_fields", None, normalized


def _non_empty_string(value: Any, *, field: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            f"案例路线字段 {field} 必须是非空字符串",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return value.strip()


def _finite_number(value: Any, *, field: str) -> int | float:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, (int, float))
        or isinstance(value, bool)
        or not math.isfinite(float(value))
    ):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            f"案例路线字段 {field} 必须是有限数值",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return value


def _normalize_design_case_workflow_input(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
    )
    if set(normalized) != {"case"} or not isinstance(
        normalized.get("case"), Mapping
    ):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            "案例路线只能包含一个严格的 case 对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case = dict(normalized["case"])
    required = {
        "cloud_id",
        "project_id",
        "case_name",
        "case_text",
        "quality_score",
        "time_score",
        "filter_threshold",
        "images",
    }
    allowed = required | set(_DESIGN_CASE_OPTIONAL_SCALAR_FIELDS)
    missing = sorted(required - set(case))
    extras = sorted(set(case) - allowed)
    if missing or extras:
        detail = []
        if missing:
            detail.append("缺少：" + ",".join(missing))
        if extras:
            detail.append("未批准：" + ",".join(extras))
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            "案例路线合同不完整（" + "；".join(detail) + "）",
            status_code=422,
        )
    cloud_id = case["cloud_id"]
    if isinstance(cloud_id, bool) or not isinstance(cloud_id, (str, int)):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            "案例 cloud_id 必须是字符串或整数",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case["cloud_id"] = _non_empty_string(str(cloud_id), field="cloud_id")
    project_id = case["project_id"]
    if isinstance(project_id, bool) or not isinstance(project_id, (str, int)):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            "案例 project_id 必须是字符串或整数",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case["project_id"] = _non_empty_string(str(project_id), field="project_id")
    case["case_name"] = _non_empty_string(case["case_name"], field="case_name")
    case["case_text"] = _non_empty_string(case["case_text"], field="case_text")
    for field in ("quality_score", "time_score", "filter_threshold"):
        case[field] = _finite_number(case[field], field=field)
    for field in _DESIGN_CASE_OPTIONAL_SCALAR_FIELDS:
        if field not in case:
            continue
        child = case[field]
        if child is None or isinstance(child, (dict, list, bool)) or not isinstance(
            child, (str, int, float)
        ):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例路线字段 {field} 必须是明确的标量值",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if isinstance(child, float) and not math.isfinite(child):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例路线字段 {field} 必须是有限值",
                status_code=422,
            )
    images = case["images"]
    if not isinstance(images, list) or not 1 <= len(images) <= 10_000:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
            "案例 images 必须包含 1 至 10000 张图片",
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized_images: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    image_fields = {"cloud_id", "oss_url", "asset_id", "asset_sha256"}
    seen_cloud_ids: set[str] = set()
    seen_asset_ids: set[int] = set()
    for index, raw_image in enumerate(images):
        if not isinstance(raw_image, Mapping) or set(raw_image) != image_fields:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例第 {index + 1} 张图片必须严格包含 cloud_id、oss_url、asset_id、asset_sha256",
                status_code=422,
            )
        image = dict(raw_image)
        raw_cloud_id = image["cloud_id"]
        if isinstance(raw_cloud_id, bool) or not isinstance(raw_cloud_id, (str, int)):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例第 {index + 1} 张图片 cloud_id 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        image_cloud_id = _non_empty_string(
            str(raw_cloud_id), field=f"images[{index}].cloud_id"
        )
        if image_cloud_id in seen_cloud_ids:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_image_duplicate",
                "同一案例内图片 cloud_id 必须唯一",
                status_code=422,
            )
        seen_cloud_ids.add(image_cloud_id)
        image["cloud_id"] = image_cloud_id
        image["oss_url"] = _non_empty_string(
            image["oss_url"], field=f"images[{index}].oss_url"
        )
        asset_id = image["asset_id"]
        if (
            not isinstance(asset_id, int)
            or isinstance(asset_id, bool)
            or asset_id <= 0
        ):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例第 {index + 1} 张图片 asset_id 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if asset_id in seen_asset_ids:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_image_duplicate",
                "同一案例内平台图片资产必须唯一",
                status_code=422,
            )
        seen_asset_ids.add(asset_id)
        sha256 = image["asset_sha256"]
        if not isinstance(sha256, str) or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", sha256) is None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_design_case_input_invalid",
                f"案例第 {index + 1} 张图片 asset_sha256 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        normalized_images.append(image)
    case["images"] = normalized_images
    return {"case": case}


def _count_map(
    value: Any,
    *,
    field: str,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    """计数字典：键是标签，值是非负整数张数。"""

    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            f"{field} 必须是「取值 → 张数」的对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for raw_key, raw_count in value.items():
        if not isinstance(raw_key, str) or not raw_key.strip():
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                f"{field} 的取值不能为空",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if isinstance(raw_count, bool) or not isinstance(raw_count, int) or raw_count < 0:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                f"{field}.{raw_key} 的张数必须是非负整数",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if raw_count:
            counts[raw_key.strip()] = raw_count
    return counts


def _normalize_design_case_aggregate_workflow_input(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """素材维度的执行输入：只有案例文本，没有图片身份。"""

    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
    )
    if set(normalized) != {"case"} or not isinstance(normalized.get("case"), Mapping):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_input_invalid",
            "素材维度路线只能包含一个严格的 case 对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case = dict(normalized["case"])
    extras = sorted(set(case) - _DESIGN_CASE_AGGREGATE_INPUT_FIELDS)
    if extras:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_input_invalid",
            "素材维度案例输入包含未批准字段：" + ",".join(extras),
            status_code=422,
        )
    case["case_name"] = _non_empty_string(case.get("case_name"), field="case_name")
    for key, child in case.items():
        if child is None or isinstance(child, (dict, list, bool)):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_input_invalid",
                f"案例字段 {key} 必须是明确的标量值",
                status_code=422,
            )
        case[key] = str(child) if not isinstance(child, str) else child
    return {"case": case}


def _normalize_design_case_dimension_workflow_input(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """图片维度路线：案例文本 + 冻结的图片身份清单。

    不复用 legacy_review 那套：那条路线要冻结 quality_score / time_score /
    filter_threshold 三个撮合参数，用来还原案例最终分；图片维度只判每张图的
    档位，不碰最终分，交付样本里也没有这三个值。硬塞默认值等于凭空造真值输入。
    """

    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
    )
    if set(normalized) != {"case"} or not isinstance(normalized.get("case"), Mapping):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
            "图片维度路线只能包含一个严格的 case 对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case = dict(normalized["case"])
    images = case.pop("images", None)
    extras = sorted(set(case) - _DESIGN_CASE_AGGREGATE_INPUT_FIELDS)
    if extras:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
            "图片维度案例输入包含未批准字段：" + ",".join(extras),
            status_code=422,
        )
    case["case_name"] = _non_empty_string(case.get("case_name"), field="case_name")
    for key, child in case.items():
        if child is None or isinstance(child, (dict, list, bool)):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
                f"案例字段 {key} 必须是明确的标量值",
                status_code=422,
            )
        case[key] = str(child) if not isinstance(child, str) else child
    if not isinstance(images, list) or not images:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
            "图片维度路线必须冻结这次要跑的图片清单",
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized_images: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen_cloud_ids: set[str] = set()
    asset_hashes: dict[int, str] = {}
    for index, raw_image in enumerate(images):
        if (
            not isinstance(raw_image, Mapping)
            or set(raw_image) != _DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_INPUT_IMAGE_FIELDS
        ):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片必须严格包含 cloud_id、asset_id、asset_sha256",
                status_code=422,
            )
        raw_cloud_id = raw_image["cloud_id"]
        if isinstance(raw_cloud_id, bool) or not isinstance(raw_cloud_id, (str, int)):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片 cloud_id 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        cloud_id = _non_empty_string(
            str(raw_cloud_id), field=f"images[{index}].cloud_id"
        )
        if cloud_id in seen_cloud_ids:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_duplicate",
                "同一案例内图片 cloud_id 必须唯一",
                status_code=422,
            )
        seen_cloud_ids.add(cloud_id)
        asset_id = raw_image["asset_id"]
        if not isinstance(asset_id, int) or isinstance(asset_id, bool) or asset_id <= 0:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片 asset_id 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        sha256 = raw_image["asset_sha256"]
        if not isinstance(sha256, str) or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", sha256) is None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_input_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片 asset_sha256 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        # 独立交付编号可以引用同一份内容，但不能给同一资产冻结冲突摘要。
        if asset_id in asset_hashes and asset_hashes[asset_id] != sha256:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_asset_hash_conflict",
                "同一图片资产的冻结内容摘要必须一致",
                status_code=422,
            )
        asset_hashes[asset_id] = sha256
        normalized_images.append(
            {"cloud_id": cloud_id, "asset_id": asset_id, "asset_sha256": sha256}
        )
    normalized_images.sort(key=lambda image: image["cloud_id"])
    case["images"] = normalized_images
    return {"case": case}


def _derive_aggregate_counts_from_text(
    normalized: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """运营按样本表原话填的三行文本，按同一套解析规则反解成计数。

    这样纠偏页不用让运营编 JSON，口径也和导入时完全一致。文本与计数同时给出
    时必须一致，否则宁可拒绝——两份不一样的真值比没有真值更危险。
    """

    from .design_case_sample_import import DesignCaseSampleError, build_image_summary

    feature_texts = normalized.get("feature_texts")
    feature_texts = feature_texts if isinstance(feature_texts, Mapping) else {}
    raw = {
        "image_total_text": normalized.get("image_total_text") or "",
        "score_distribution_text": normalized.get("score_distribution_text") or "",
        "type_distribution_text": feature_texts.get("trait") or "",
    }
    if not any(str(text).strip() for text in raw.values()):
        return normalized
    try:
        derived = build_image_summary({k: str(v) for k, v in raw.items()})
    except DesignCaseSampleError as exc:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            str(exc),
            status_code=422,
        ) from exc
    for field in ("image_total", "score_distribution", "feature_counts"):
        given = normalized.get(field)
        if given in (None, {}, 0):
            continue
        if given != derived[field]:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_conflict",
                f"素材维度真值的 {field} 与文字描述不一致，请只改其中一处",
                status_code=422,
            )
    merged = dict(normalized)
    merged.update(
        {
            "image_total": derived["image_total"],
            "score_distribution": derived["score_distribution"],
            "scored_images": derived["scored_images"],
            "feature_counts": derived["feature_counts"],
        }
    )
    return merged


def normalize_design_case_aggregate_expected_result(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """冻结素材维度真值：图片总数、分数分布、各特征取值计数。"""

    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
    )
    extras = sorted(set(normalized) - _DESIGN_CASE_AGGREGATE_RESULT_FIELDS)
    if extras:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            "素材维度真值包含未登记字段：" + ",".join(extras),
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized = _derive_aggregate_counts_from_text(normalized)
    total = normalized.get("image_total")
    if isinstance(total, bool) or not isinstance(total, int) or total <= 0:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            "素材维度真值必须包含正整数的图片总张数",
            status_code=422,
        )
    scores = _count_map(
        normalized.get("score_distribution") or {},
        field="score_distribution",
    )
    scored = sum(scores.values())
    if scored > total:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            f"分数分布合计 {scored} 张超过图片总数 {total} 张",
            status_code=422,
        )
    features = normalized.get("feature_counts") or {}
    if not isinstance(features, Mapping):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            "feature_counts 必须是「特征名 → 计数」的对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized_features: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for name, counts in features.items():
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                "特征名不能为空",
                status_code=422,
            )
        entry = _count_map(counts, field=f"feature_counts.{name}")
        # 特征是多值的：一张图可以同时是「分析图」和「3D数字效果图」，平台
        # aggregate_case 对每个取值各记一次，所以各取值之和天然可以大于图片
        # 总数（2026-09-09 在案例交付样本 中差_895798 上实测 32 > 30）。
        # 能约束的是单个取值——同一个类型不可能出现在比总数还多的图片上。
        overflowing = sorted(
            value for value, count in entry.items() if count > total
        )
        if overflowing:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                f"特征 {name} 的取值 {','.join(overflowing)} 超过图片总数 {total} 张",
                status_code=422,
            )
        normalized_features[name.strip()] = entry
    normalized["score_distribution"] = scores
    normalized["scored_images"] = scored
    normalized["feature_counts"] = normalized_features
    for text_key in ("image_total_text", "score_distribution_text"):
        text = normalized.get(text_key)
        if text is not None and not isinstance(text, str):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                f"{text_key} 必须是字符串",
                status_code=422,
            )
    texts = normalized.get("feature_texts")
    if texts is not None and (
        not isinstance(texts, Mapping)
        or any(not isinstance(v, str) for v in texts.values())
    ):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
            "feature_texts 必须是「特征名 → 文本」的对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return normalized


def normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    workflow_input: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """冻结图片维度真值：案例一档，加上冻结图片清单里每一张的人工档位。

    逐图档位必须覆盖冻结清单里的每一张、且不能多出清单外的图片。少一张就说明
    这份真值和这次要跑的图不是同一批，回归出来的准确率无从解释；宁可导入时拒绝。
    """

    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
    )
    extras = sorted(set(normalized) - _DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_RESULT_FIELDS)
    if extras:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
            "图片维度真值包含未登记字段：" + ",".join(extras),
            status_code=422,
        )
    level = normalized.get("level")
    if level not in _LEVELS:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
            "案例档位必须是 L1-L5",
            status_code=422,
        )
    frozen_case = workflow_input.get("case")
    frozen_case = frozen_case if isinstance(frozen_case, Mapping) else {}
    frozen_images = frozen_case.get("images")
    frozen_ids = [
        str(image.get("cloud_id"))
        for image in (frozen_images if isinstance(frozen_images, list) else [])
        if isinstance(image, Mapping) and image.get("cloud_id") is not None
    ]
    if not frozen_ids:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
            "图片维度真值必须挂在已冻结图片清单的案例上",
            status_code=422,
        )
    raw_images = normalized.get("images")
    if not isinstance(raw_images, list) or not raw_images:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
            "图片维度真值必须逐张给出档位",
            status_code=422,
        )
    graded: dict[str, str] = {}
    for index, raw_image in enumerate(raw_images):
        if not isinstance(raw_image, Mapping):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片真值必须是对象",
                status_code=422,
            )
        image_extras = sorted(set(raw_image) - _DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_IMAGE_FIELDS)
        if image_extras:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片真值包含未登记字段：" + ",".join(image_extras),
                status_code=422,
            )
        raw_cloud_id = raw_image.get("cloud_id")
        if isinstance(raw_cloud_id, bool) or not isinstance(raw_cloud_id, (str, int)):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片真值 cloud_id 非法",
                status_code=422,
            )
        cloud_id = _non_empty_string(
            str(raw_cloud_id), field=f"images[{index}].cloud_id"
        )
        image_level = raw_image.get("level")
        if image_level == "L5":
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_image_level_l5_forbidden",
                "图片维度没有过滤档：单张图片只能是 L1-L4",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if image_level not in DESIGN_CASE_IMAGE_LEVELS:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 张图片档位必须是 L1-L4",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if cloud_id in graded:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                f"图片 {cloud_id} 的档位重复给出",
                status_code=422,
            )
        graded[cloud_id] = image_level
    frozen_set = set(frozen_ids)
    unknown = sorted(set(graded) - frozen_set)
    if unknown:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_unknown_image",
            "图片维度真值包含冻结清单外的图片：" + ",".join(unknown),
            status_code=422,
        )
    ungraded = sorted(frozen_set - set(graded))
    if ungraded:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_dimension_truth_incomplete",
            "冻结清单里还有图片没有档位：" + ",".join(ungraded),
            status_code=422,
        )
    normalized["level"] = level
    normalized["images"] = [
        {"cloud_id": cloud_id, "level": graded[cloud_id]}
        for cloud_id in sorted(graded)
    ]
    return normalized


def _normalize_workflow_input(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    truth_contract_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        return _normalize_design_case_workflow_input(value)
    if truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        return _normalize_design_case_dimension_workflow_input(value)
    if truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_AGGREGATE_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        return _normalize_design_case_aggregate_workflow_input(value)
    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
    )
    forbidden = sorted(set(normalized) - _WORKFLOW_INPUT_FIELDS)
    if forbidden:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_workflow_input_forbidden",
            "人工真值路线包含未批准字段：" + ",".join(forbidden),
            status_code=422,
        )
    for key, child in normalized.items():
        if isinstance(child, (dict, list)) or child is None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
                f"路线字段 {key} 必须是明确的标量值",
                status_code=422,
            )
    source = normalized.get("source")
    if "is_single" in normalized:
        value = normalized["is_single"]
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            value = int(value)
        # 海外 is_single 编码 2026-09-02 由 0/1 改为 1/2；合同键已写明路线的
        # 历史真值按路线自愈到当前编码，避免旧基准集读取即报错。
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(truth_contract_key)
        if contract is not None:
            healed = normalize_is_single_for_contract(contract, value)
            if healed is None:
                raise BaselineTruthError(
                    "baseline_truth_scope_invalid",
                    f"整体/单体取值 {value!r} 与路线 {truth_contract_key} 不符",
                    status_code=422,
                )
            value = healed
        elif (
            not isinstance(source, str)
            or asset_scope_from_is_single(source, value) is None
        ):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_scope_invalid",
                "整体/单体只能选择该站点允许的明确值（国内 0/1，海外 1/2）",
                status_code=422,
            )
        normalized["is_single"] = value
    has_route_identity = any(
        key in normalized for key in ("source", "is_single", "ll_id", "res_id")
    )
    if has_route_identity:
        if source not in {"domestic", "overseas"} or "is_single" not in normalized:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_route_invalid",
                "路线必须同时声明国内/海外与整体/单体",
                status_code=422,
            )
        identity_key = "ll_id" if source == "domestic" else "res_id"
        incompatible_key = "res_id" if source == "domestic" else "ll_id"
        identity = normalized.get(identity_key)
        if not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip():
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_route_identity_missing",
                f"当前路线必须填写 {identity_key}",
                status_code=422,
            )
        if incompatible_key in normalized:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_route_identity_conflict",
                f"当前路线不能同时携带 {incompatible_key}",
                status_code=422,
            )
        normalized[identity_key] = identity.strip()
    fixture_key = normalized.get("fixture_key")
    if fixture_key is not None:
        if not isinstance(fixture_key, str) or not fixture_key.strip():
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "baseline_truth_fixture_key_invalid",
                "测试夹具标识不能为空",
                status_code=422,
            )
        normalized["fixture_key"] = fixture_key.strip()
    return normalized


def normalize_baseline_workflow_input(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    truth_contract_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate one versioned, server-owned baseline execution input."""

    return _normalize_workflow_input(
        value,
        truth_contract_key=truth_contract_key,
    )


def normalize_design_case_expected_result(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    workflow_input: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Freeze only leaf paths that exist in the terminal case result schema."""

    normalized = _canonical_object(
        value,
        code="design_case_truth_result_invalid",
    )
    allowed_root = {"case_cloud_id", "level", "final_score", "case_review"}
    if set(normalized) - allowed_root:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
            "案例真值包含不属于终态 normalized_result 的顶层字段",
            status_code=422,
        )
    case_cloud_id = normalized.get("case_cloud_id")
    case_input = workflow_input.get("case")
    frozen_cloud_id = (
        case_input.get("cloud_id") if isinstance(case_input, Mapping) else None
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(case_cloud_id, str)
        or not case_cloud_id.strip()
        or case_cloud_id != frozen_cloud_id
    ):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
            "案例真值 case_cloud_id 必须与冻结案例输入一致",
            status_code=422,
        )
    level = normalized.get("level")
    if level not in _LEVELS:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
            "案例真值必须包含 L1-L5 最终等级",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if "final_score" in normalized:
        _finite_number(normalized["final_score"], field="final_score")

    review = normalized.get("case_review")
    if review is not None:
        if not isinstance(review, Mapping):
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
                "案例 case_review 真值必须是对象",
                status_code=422,
            )
        review = dict(review)
        allowed_review = {
            field["key"].split(".", 1)[1]
            for field in DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_FIELDS
            if str(field["key"]).startswith("case_review.")
        }
        if set(review) - allowed_review:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
                "案例 case_review 包含未登记字段",
                status_code=422,
            )
        try:
            validate_design_case_truth_review(review)
        except PromptOutputContractError as exc:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
                "案例 case_review 不符合冻结的严格输出字段合同",
                status_code=422,
            ) from exc
        normalized["case_review"] = review
    return normalized


def _normalize_contract_truth(
    *,
    expected_level: str | None,
    expected_result: Mapping[str, Any],
    truth_contract_key: str,
    workflow_input: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str, str | None, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply the versioned truth contract before deriving the storage kind."""

    if truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        if expected_level is not None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_truth_result_invalid",
                "案例真值等级必须冻结在结构化结果的 level 字段中",
                status_code=422,
            )
        expected_result = normalize_design_case_expected_result(
            expected_result,
            workflow_input=workflow_input,
        )
    elif truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        if expected_level is not None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_dimension_truth_invalid",
                "图片维度的案例档位必须冻结在结构化结果的 level 字段中",
                status_code=422,
            )
        expected_result = normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result(
            expected_result,
            workflow_input=workflow_input,
        )
    elif truth_contract_key == DESIGN_CASE_AGGREGATE_TRUTH_CONTRACT:
        if expected_level is not None:
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "design_case_aggregate_truth_invalid",
                "素材维度真值是图片统计，不使用 L1-L5 等级",
                status_code=422,
            )
        expected_result = normalize_design_case_aggregate_expected_result(
            expected_result
        )
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(truth_contract_key)
    if contract is not None and "is_single" in expected_result:
        # 结构化真值里的 is_single 同样按路线自愈（海外历史 0/1 → 1/2），
        # 否则指标层会把模型正确输出的新编码判成字段错误。
        healed = normalize_is_single_for_contract(
            contract, expected_result.get("is_single")
        )
        if healed is not None:
            expected_result = {**expected_result, "is_single": healed}
    return _normalize_truth(
        expected_level=expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
    )


def _hash_payload(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(dict(payload)).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _truth_payload(
    *,
    item_id: int,
    revision: int,
    truth_kind: str,
    expected_level: str | None,
    expected_result: Mapping[str, Any],
    truth_contract_key: str,
    workflow_input: Mapping[str, Any],
    previous_hash: str | None,
    source_baseline_run_id: int | None,
    source_production_run_id: int | None,
    source_correction_key: str | None,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-truth-revision-v1",
        "baseline_set_item_id": item_id,
        "revision": revision,
        "truth_kind": truth_kind,
        "expected_level": expected_level,
        "expected_result": dict(expected_result),
        "truth_contract_key": truth_contract_key,
        "workflow_input": dict(workflow_input),
        "previous_hash": previous_hash,
        "source_baseline_run_id": source_baseline_run_id,
        "source_production_run_id": source_production_run_id,
        "source_correction_key": source_correction_key,
        "reason": reason,
        "actor": actor,
    }


def _original_truth(item: BaselineSetItem) -> EffectiveBaselineTruth:
    expected_result = _json_object(
        item.expected_result_json,
        code="baseline_truth_result_invalid",
        message="导入的结构化人工真值已损坏",
    )
    if not expected_result and item.expected_level is not None:
        expected_result = {"level": item.expected_level}
    contract_key = str(
        item.truth_contract_key
        or ("level.v1" if item.expected_level is not None else "")
    ).strip()
    if not contract_key:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_contract_missing",
            "导入的人工真值缺少合同标识",
            status_code=422,
        )
    asset_snapshot = _json_object(
        item.asset_snapshot_json,
        code="baseline_truth_asset_snapshot_invalid",
        message="导入素材的冻结快照已损坏",
    )
    raw_input = asset_snapshot.get("workflow_input", {})
    if not isinstance(raw_input, Mapping):
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
            "导入素材的工作流路线必须是对象",
            status_code=422,
        )
    workflow_input = _normalize_workflow_input(
        raw_input,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
    )
    truth_kind, expected_level, expected_result = _normalize_contract_truth(
        expected_level=item.expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
    )
    payload = _truth_payload(
        item_id=item.id,
        revision=0,
        truth_kind=truth_kind,
        expected_level=expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
        previous_hash=None,
        source_baseline_run_id=None,
        source_production_run_id=None,
        source_correction_key=None,
        reason="baseline_import",
        actor="baseline_import",
    )
    return EffectiveBaselineTruth(
        source="baseline_import",
        truth_kind=truth_kind,
        expected_level=expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
        revision=0,
        revision_id=None,
        canonical_hash=_hash_payload(payload),
    )


def resolve_effective_baseline_truth(
    db: Session,
    item: BaselineSetItem,
) -> EffectiveBaselineTruth:
    latest = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineTruthRevision)
        .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == item.id)
        .order_by(BaselineTruthRevision.revision.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    return effective_truth_from_latest(item, latest)


def effective_truth_from_latest(
    item: BaselineSetItem,
    latest: BaselineTruthRevision | None,
) -> EffectiveBaselineTruth:
    """同 resolve_effective_baseline_truth，最新修订由调用方批量取好传入（分页读逐条结果用）。"""
    if latest is None:
        return _original_truth(item)
    expected_result = _json_object(
        latest.expected_result_json,
        code="baseline_truth_result_invalid",
        message="人工真值修订内容已损坏",
    )
    workflow_input = _normalize_workflow_input(
        _json_object(
            latest.workflow_input_json,
            code="baseline_truth_workflow_input_invalid",
            message="人工真值路线内容已损坏",
        ),
        truth_contract_key=latest.truth_contract_key,
    )
    truth_kind, expected_level, expected_result = _normalize_contract_truth(
        expected_level=latest.expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=latest.truth_contract_key,
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
    )
    if truth_kind != latest.truth_kind:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_kind_mismatch",
            "人工真值修订类型与内容不一致",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return EffectiveBaselineTruth(
        source="revision",
        truth_kind=latest.truth_kind,
        expected_level=expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=latest.truth_contract_key,
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
        revision=latest.revision,
        revision_id=latest.id,
        canonical_hash=latest.canonical_hash,
    )


def append_baseline_truth_revision(
    db: Session,
    *,
    item: BaselineSetItem,
    expected_level: str | None,
    expected_result: Mapping[str, Any],
    truth_contract_key: str,
    workflow_input: Mapping[str, Any],
    source_baseline_run_id: int | None,
    source_production_run_id: int | None,
    source_correction_key: str | None,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> BaselineTruthRevision:
    contract_key = str(truth_contract_key or "").strip()
    normalized_input = _normalize_workflow_input(
        workflow_input,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
    )
    truth_kind, normalized_level, normalized_result = _normalize_contract_truth(
        expected_level=expected_level,
        expected_result=expected_result,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input=normalized_input,
    )
    normalized_reason = str(reason or "").strip()
    normalized_actor = str(actor or "").strip()
    correction_key = (
        str(source_correction_key).strip()
        if source_correction_key is not None
        else None
    )
    if not contract_key or not normalized_reason or not normalized_actor:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_provenance_missing",
            "人工真值合同、原因和操作人不能为空",
            status_code=422,
        )
    if source_correction_key is not None and not correction_key:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_correction_key_invalid",
            "纠偏幂等键不能为空",
            status_code=422,
        )

    if correction_key is not None:
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(BaselineTruthRevision)
            .where(BaselineTruthRevision.source_correction_key == correction_key)
            .with_for_update()
        )
        if existing is not None:
            exact_replay = (
                existing.baseline_set_item_id == item.id
                and existing.truth_kind == truth_kind
                and existing.expected_level == normalized_level
                and existing.expected_result_json == canonical_json(normalized_result)
                and existing.truth_contract_key == contract_key
                and existing.workflow_input_json == canonical_json(normalized_input)
                and existing.source_baseline_run_id == source_baseline_run_id
                and existing.source_production_run_id == source_production_run_id
                and existing.reason == normalized_reason
                and existing.actor == normalized_actor
            )
            if exact_replay:
                return existing
            raise BaselineTruthError(
                "truth_revision_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同纠偏幂等键已绑定不同的人工真值",
            )

    locked_item = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineSetItem)
        .where(BaselineSetItem.id == item.id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if locked_item is None:
        raise BaselineTruthError(
            "baseline_truth_item_missing",
            "需要修订的存量素材不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    latest = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineTruthRevision)
        .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == locked_item.id)
        .order_by(BaselineTruthRevision.revision.desc())
        .limit(1)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if latest is None:
        previous_hash = _original_truth(locked_item).canonical_hash
        revision = 1
    else:
        previous_hash = latest.canonical_hash
        revision = latest.revision + 1
    payload = _truth_payload(
        item_id=locked_item.id,
        revision=revision,
        truth_kind=truth_kind,
        expected_level=normalized_level,
        expected_result=normalized_result,
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input=normalized_input,
        previous_hash=previous_hash,
        source_baseline_run_id=source_baseline_run_id,
        source_production_run_id=source_production_run_id,
        source_correction_key=correction_key,
        reason=normalized_reason,
        actor=normalized_actor,
    )
    row = BaselineTruthRevision(
        baseline_set_item_id=locked_item.id,
        revision=revision,
        truth_kind=truth_kind,
        expected_level=normalized_level,
        expected_result_json=canonical_json(normalized_result),
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        workflow_input_json=canonical_json(normalized_input),
        source_baseline_run_id=source_baseline_run_id,
        source_production_run_id=source_production_run_id,
        source_correction_key=correction_key,
        reason=normalized_reason,
        actor=normalized_actor,
        previous_hash=previous_hash,
        canonical_hash=_hash_payload(payload),
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：真值修订后同事务刷新；失败只记日志。
    from .wide_material import refresh_material

    refresh_material(db, locked_item.asset_id, event_kind="truth_revised")
    return row

===== FILE backend/app/baseline_truth_import.py =====
"""把运营改过的导出表读回来，落成真值修订。

嘉欣的用法（2026-09-08）：导出 → 离线对着缩略图逐条核对中英翻译 → 在表里
把「真值」列改对 → 传回平台。模型列是大模型的历史输出、不可改写，只有真值
列是人工校正的结论。

三条硬规则，都是为了不让一次误操作毁掉真值：
1. **只读真值列**。模型列即使被改了也一律忽略——那是历史事实。
2. **留空 = 不修改**。运营漏填一格不该把已有真值抹掉；要显式清空得填 `-`。
3. **必须先预览**。逐条列出「改哪个字段、从什么改成什么」，确认后才写。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence

from .baseline_export import NOT_COMPARED, UNFILLED_TRUTH
from .knowledge_graph_contracts import field_label, get_knowledge_graph_contract
from .p0e_safe_import import XlsxLimits, read_xlsx_table

ID_HEADER = "条目ID"
TRUTH_SUFFIX = "（真值）"
MODEL_SUFFIX = "（模型）"
# 显式清空的标记：只有填了它才会把已有真值改成空
CLEAR_MARKER = "-"
# 导出时写出去的占位符，读回来一律当「没改」
IGNORED_VALUES = {"", UNFILLED_TRUTH, NOT_COMPARED}

_IS_SINGLE_CELL = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d+)")


class BaselineTruthImportError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass
class TruthChange:
    row_number: int
    item_id: int
    field: str
    label: str
    before: Any
    after: Any


@dataclass
class ParsedTruthSheet:
    changes: list[TruthChange] = field(default_factory=list)
    unchanged_rows: int = 0
    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    columns: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _text(value: Any) -> str:
    return "" if value is None else str(value).strip()


def _label_to_field(contract_keys: Sequence[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """中文列名 → 字段名。用该轮出现过的路线的字段建表。"""

    mapping: dict[str, str] = {}
    for key in contract_keys:
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(key)
        if contract is None:
            continue
        for name in contract.field_order:
            mapping[field_label(name)] = name
    return mapping


def parse_is_single_cell(raw: str) -> int | None:
    """真值列里的 is_single 形如「1 整体」或「1 整体（原值 0）」，取前面的码。"""

    matched = _IS_SINGLE_CELL.match(raw)
    return int(matched.group(1)) if matched else None


def _normalize_truth_cell(field_name: str, raw: str) -> Any | None:
    """把单元格转成要写入的真值；返回 None 表示「不修改」。"""

    if raw in IGNORED_VALUES:
        return None
    if raw == CLEAR_MARKER:
        return ""
    if field_name == "is_single":
        code = parse_is_single_cell(raw)
        if code is None:
            raise BaselineTruthImportError(
                "baseline_truth_import_is_single_invalid",
                f"整体/单体填写无法识别：{raw}",
            )
        return code
    return raw


def parse_truth_sheet(
    path: str | Path,
    *,
    current_truth: Mapping[int, Mapping[str, Any]],
    contract_keys: Sequence[str],
    limits: XlsxLimits | None = None,
) -> ParsedTruthSheet:
    """解析改过的导出表，只挑出真正有变化的真值单元格。

    ``current_truth`` 是各条目当前生效的真值，用来算差异——只有和现状不同的
    才算一次修改，避免运营原样传回时凭空产生一堆空修订。
    """

    table = read_xlsx_table(path, limits=limits)
    if not table.rows:
        raise BaselineTruthImportError("baseline_truth_import_empty", "表格没有数据")
    header_values = table.rows[0][1]
    headers = {
        _text(header_values.get(column)): column
        for column in range(1, table.last_column + 1)
    }
    if ID_HEADER not in headers:
        raise BaselineTruthImportError(
            "baseline_truth_import_id_column_missing",
            f"表格缺少「{ID_HEADER}」列，无法定位条目；请用平台导出的表格修改后上传",
        )
    label_map = _label_to_field(contract_keys)
    truth_columns: dict[int, str] = {}
    for header, column in headers.items():
        if not header.endswith(TRUTH_SUFFIX):
            continue
        name = label_map.get(header[: -len(TRUTH_SUFFIX)])
        if name is not None:
            truth_columns[column] = name
    if not truth_columns:
        raise BaselineTruthImportError(
            "baseline_truth_import_no_truth_columns",
            "表格里没有可识别的真值列",
        )

    result = ParsedTruthSheet(columns={"id": headers[ID_HEADER]})
    for row_number, values in table.data_rows:
        raw_id = _text(values.get(headers[ID_HEADER]))
        if not raw_id:
            continue
        if not raw_id.isdigit():
            result.skipped.append(
                {"row_number": row_number, "reason": f"条目ID 不是数字：{raw_id}"}
            )
            continue
        item_id = int(raw_id)
        current = current_truth.get(item_id)
        if current is None:
            result.skipped.append(
                {"row_number": row_number, "reason": f"条目 {item_id} 不属于本轮，已跳过"}
            )
            continue
        row_changes: list[TruthChange] = []
        for column, name in truth_columns.items():
            raw = _text(values.get(column))
            try:
