                value = _normalize_truth_cell(name, raw)
            except BaselineTruthImportError as exc:
                result.skipped.append(
                    {"row_number": row_number, "reason": f"{field_label(name)}：{exc}"}
                )
                row_changes = []
                break
            if value is None:
                continue
            before = current.get(name)
            if isinstance(before, str) and isinstance(value, str):
                if before.strip() == value.strip():
                    continue
            elif before == value:
                continue
            row_changes.append(
                TruthChange(
                    row_number=row_number, item_id=item_id, field=name,
                    label=field_label(name), before=before, after=value,
                )
            )
        if row_changes:
            result.changes.extend(row_changes)
        else:
            result.unchanged_rows += 1
    return result


def audit_summary(result: Mapping[str, Any], *, top: int = 10, ids: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """审计里除了计数还要留失败原因：2026-09-18 运营导入 248 条未写入，
    界面关掉后原因就没了，事后只剩「failed_items: 248」查不了。"""

    from collections import Counter

    failed = list(result.get("failed") or [])
    reasons = Counter(str(item.get("reason", ""))[:120] for item in failed)
    ignored = Counter(str(item.get("reason", ""))[:120] for item in (result.get("ignored") or []))
    return {
        **dict(result.get("summary") or {}),
        "failed_reasons": [
            {"reason": reason, "count": count} for reason, count in reasons.most_common(top)
        ],
        "failed_item_ids": [item.get("item_id") for item in failed][:ids],
        "ignored_reasons": [
            {"reason": reason, "count": count} for reason, count in ignored.most_common(top)
        ],
    }


def preview_payload(parsed: ParsedTruthSheet, *, limit: int = 200) -> dict[str, Any]:
    by_item: dict[int, list[TruthChange]] = {}
    for change in parsed.changes:
        by_item.setdefault(change.item_id, []).append(change)
    items = [
        {
            "item_id": item_id,
            "row_number": changes[0].row_number,
            "changes": [
                {
                    "field": c.field, "label": c.label,
                    "before": c.before, "after": c.after,
                }
                for c in changes
            ],
        }
        for item_id, changes in sorted(by_item.items())
    ]
    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-truth-import-preview-v1",
        "writes_business_database": False,
        "summary": {
            "changed_items": len(by_item),
            "changed_fields": len(parsed.changes),
            "unchanged_rows": parsed.unchanged_rows,
            "skipped_rows": len(parsed.skipped),
        },
        "items": items[:limit],
        "truncated": len(items) > limit,
        "skipped": parsed.skipped[:limit],
    }


def load_run_truth(db, run_id: int) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, Any]], list[str], dict[int, str]]:
    """装载一轮各条目当前生效的真值，返回 (真值映射, 路线集合, 条目→合同)。

    键是 baseline_set_item_id——真值挂在基准集条目上，不是回归条目上。
    改真值影响的是**下一轮**的比对基准，这正是运营要的语义。
    """

    import json as _json

    from sqlalchemy import select as _select

    from .baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from .models import BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineSetItem

    rows = db.scalars(
        _select(BaselineRegressionItem)
        .where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run_id)
        .order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id.asc())
    ).all()
    truth: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    contracts: dict[int, str] = {}
    keys: list[str] = []
    for row in rows:
        if row.baseline_set_item_id is None:
            continue
        if row.truth_contract_key and row.truth_contract_key not in keys:
            keys.append(row.truth_contract_key)
        item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, row.baseline_set_item_id)
        if item is None:
            continue
        try:
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            current = dict(effective.expected_result or {})
            contract_key = effective.truth_contract_key or row.truth_contract_key or ""
        except Exception:
            # 真值读不出来的条目不参与回填，但不该让整批失败
            current = _json.loads(row.expected_result_json or "{}")
            contract_key = row.truth_contract_key or ""
        truth[row.baseline_set_item_id] = current
        contracts[row.baseline_set_item_id] = contract_key
    return truth, keys, contracts


def apply_changes(
    db,
    parsed: ParsedTruthSheet,
    *,
    contracts: Mapping[int, str],
    current_truth: Mapping[int, Mapping[str, Any]],
    actor: str,
    edit_key: str,
    reason: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把差异逐条落成追加式真值修订。

    一条写失败不影响其余：运营改了 80 条，不该因为第 3 条不合合同就全军覆没。
    失败逐条留原因，前端照实展示。
    """

    from .baseline_truth import (
        BaselineTruthError,
        append_baseline_truth_revision,
        resolve_effective_baseline_truth,
    )
    from .knowledge_graph_contracts import (
        KnowledgeGraphContractError,
        validate_knowledge_graph_output,
    )
    from .models import BaselineSetItem

    by_item: dict[int, list[TruthChange]] = {}
    for change in parsed.changes:
        by_item.setdefault(change.item_id, []).append(change)

    # 本轮出现过的所有路线的字段并集 = 导出表可能给出的列。只有落在这个并集里、
    # 但不属于本条目路线的列才算「并集列」可以忽略；不属于任何路线的字段名（列名被
    # 改坏、伪造字段）仍然必须失败，否则一次误操作会被静默吞掉。
    union_fields: set[str] = set()
    for key in set(contracts.values()):
        known = get_knowledge_graph_contract(key)
        if known is not None:
            union_fields.update(known.field_order)

    applied: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    failed: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    ignored: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item_id, changes in sorted(by_item.items()):
        item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, item_id)
        contract_key = contracts.get(item_id) or ""
        if item is None or not contract_key:
            failed.append({"item_id": item_id, "reason": "条目或字段合同不存在"})
            continue
        # 导出表的真值列是本轮所有路线的并集（海外有 res_id/title、国内有 ll_id），
        # 但每个条目只归属一条路线。运营把不属于自己路线的列留空，`_normalize_truth_cell`
        # 会返回 ""（= 清空）而不是 None（= 不修改），合并进 payload 后合同就报
        # 「输出包含多余字段」——2026-09-18 运营导入 248 条未写入，其中 200 条是这个。
        # 身份字段与 is_single 也不是真值：前者必须等于素材 ID，后者是素材路线，
        # 改了只会和冻结合同冲突。三类一律按「已忽略」记录，不让整条失败。
        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key)
        editable = (
            {name for name in contract.field_order}
            - {contract.identity_field, "is_single"}
            if contract is not None
            else None
        )
        if editable is not None:
            kept: list[TruthChange] = []
            for change in changes:
                if change.field in editable:
                    kept.append(change)
                    continue
                if change.field not in union_fields:
                    # 不是并集列，是真异常：交给下面的合同校验照常失败。
                    kept.append(change)
                    continue
                ignored.append(
                    {
                        "item_id": item_id,
                        "row_number": change.row_number,
                        "field": change.field,
                        "label": change.label,
                        "reason": (
                            f"{contract.identity_field} 必须等于素材 ID，不能在真值表里改"
                            if change.field == contract.identity_field
                            else "整体/单体由素材路线决定，不能在真值表里改"
                            if change.field == "is_single"
                            else f"该列不属于本条目的路线（{contract_key}），已忽略"
                        ),
                    }
                )
            changes = kept
            if not changes:
                continue
        merged = dict(current_truth.get(item_id) or {})
        for change in changes:
            merged[change.field] = change.after
        try:
            # 每条一个 SAVEPOINT。原先这里失败走 db.rollback()，而那是整事务回滚：
            # 前面已经 flush 的修订会被一起抹掉，只有「最后一次失败之后」的几条能活到 commit。
            # 回执里的 applied_items 只是列表长度、不受回滚影响，审计又在函数返回后才写，
            # 于是界面显示「已写入 147 条」而库里一条都没有（2026-09-17 正式服实证）。
            with db.begin_nested():
                effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
                identity = merged.get(
                    (get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key).identity_field)
                    if get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key) else ""
                )
                validated = validate_knowledge_graph_output(
                    contract_key,
                    merged,
                    expected_asset_id=identity if isinstance(identity, str) else "",
                )
                append_baseline_truth_revision(
                    db,
                    item=item,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result=validated,
                    truth_contract_key=contract_key,
                    workflow_input=effective.workflow_input,
                    source_baseline_run_id=None,
                    source_production_run_id=None,
                    source_correction_key=f"{edit_key}:{item_id}",
                    reason=reason,
                    actor=actor,
                )
            applied.append(
                {
                    "item_id": item_id,
                    "fields": [c.field for c in changes],
                }
            )
        except (BaselineTruthError, KnowledgeGraphContractError) as exc:
            failed.append({"item_id": item_id, "reason": str(exc)[:300]})
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            failed.append({"item_id": item_id, "reason": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]})
    return {
        "schema_version": "baseline-truth-import-result-v1",
        "summary": {
            "applied_items": len(applied),
            "applied_fields": sum(len(a["fields"]) for a in applied),
            "failed_items": len(failed),
            "ignored_fields": len(ignored),
            "unchanged_rows": parsed.unchanged_rows,
            "skipped_rows": len(parsed.skipped),
        },
        "applied": applied,
        "failed": failed,
        "ignored": ignored[:200],
        "skipped": parsed.skipped[:200],
    }

===== FILE backend/app/batch_endpoint_recovery.py =====
"""Frozen batch-endpoint recovery policy; no Batch Job or TOS dependency."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Mapping

VERSION = 'batch-online-fallback-v1'
DEADLINE_SECONDS = 86400
BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3
RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE = 'model_provider_resource_wait'
BUDGET_WAIT_CODE = 'model_budget_wait'
DURABLE_WAIT_CODES = frozenset({RESOURCE_WAIT_CODE, BUDGET_WAIT_CODE,
    'model_batch_submission_wait', 'model_batch_experiment_hold',
    'model_failure_streak_exhausted'})
BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_CODES = frozenset({
    'model_budget_requests_exhausted', 'model_budget_input_exhausted',
    'model_budget_output_exhausted', 'model_budget_cost_exhausted',
    'incremental_daily_requests_exhausted', 'incremental_daily_input_exhausted',
    'incremental_daily_output_exhausted', 'incremental_daily_cost_exhausted',
})

# These failures are owned by the frozen per-node batch/online attempt budget,
# not the aggregate's permanent-failure breaker. Keep dispatch and admission
# classification together so a valid retry cannot be killed by sibling errors.
RECOVERABLE_FAILURE_CODES = frozenset({
    'model_provider_429', 'model_provider_transient_parse', 'model_provider_json_truncated',
    'structured_output_schema_mismatch', 'model_output_semantic_inconsistency',
})


def utc(value):
    if not isinstance(value, datetime):
        raise ValueError('material queue timestamp missing')
    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    mode: str
    attempt_no: int | None
    remaining_seconds: float
    reservation_no: int = 1


def decide(*, queued_at, now, batch_count, online_count, online_limit, online_started=False,
           online_allowed=True):
    if (any(type(v) is not int or v < 0 for v in (batch_count, online_count))
            or type(online_limit) is not int or not 1 <= online_limit <= 100):
        raise ValueError('invalid frozen transport attempt limits')
    remaining = max(0.0, DEADLINE_SECONDS-(utc(now)-utc(queued_at)).total_seconds())
    if online_count >= online_limit:
        return Decision('exhausted', None, remaining)
    if online_started or online_count or batch_count >= BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS or remaining <= 0:
        if not online_allowed:
            # Not entitled to the online endpoint: the batch attempts are spent,
            # so this is exhausted, which fails the material the ordinary way.
            # Deliberately not 'held' - a hold retries every 15 minutes forever
            # and would occupy an in-flight slot that nothing can ever release.
            return Decision('exhausted', None, remaining)
        return Decision('online', online_count+1, remaining)
    return Decision('batch', batch_count+1, remaining)


def is_resource_rejection(row):
    """Only a confirmed HTTP 429 is exempt from evaluation attempts.

    The provider refused the request at the entrance, so nothing was billed:
    reserve_model_invocation also leaves it out of the frozen budget
    projection. The ledger row itself, with its reservation evidence, stays.
    """
    return (row.status == 'failed' and row.error_code == 'model_provider_429'
            and row.upstream_status_code == 429)


def is_operator_interruption(row):
    """Server-recorded maintenance is not a failed model evaluation.

    Only batch-only experiments use this classification. The physical call
    remains charged and gets a new reservation identity on retry.
    """
    if row.status != 'failed' or row.error_code != 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown':
        return False
    try:
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json or '{}')
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False
    failure = evidence.get('failure') if isinstance(evidence, Mapping) else None
    return (isinstance(failure, Mapping) and failure.get('retryable') is True
        and failure.get('technical_error_type') == 'operator_interrupted'
        and isinstance(failure.get('operator_incident'), str)
        and failure['operator_incident'].startswith('baseline:'))


def is_wait_cap_timeout(row):
    """Local provider-wait cap hit (ADR-0084): unknown outcome, fee retained.

    Only batch-only experiments treat this like a resource wait rather than a
    failed evaluation; the physical call keeps its ledger identity and cost.
    """
    if row.status != 'failed' or row.error_code != 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown':
        return False
    try:
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json or '{}')
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False
    failure = evidence.get('failure') if isinstance(evidence, Mapping) else None
    return (isinstance(failure, Mapping) and failure.get('retryable') is True
        and failure.get('technical_error_type') == 'timeout'
        and failure.get('wait_cap_hit') is True
        and type(failure.get('wait_cap_seconds')) in {int, float}
        and failure['wait_cap_seconds'] >= 360)


ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_ONLINE_FALLBACK_ESCALATION'


def online_escalation_enabled(environ=None):
    """部署级总开关：满 24 小时或批量尝试用尽之后还转不转在线兜底。

    2026-09-17 老大要求先关掉这条策略（在线单价是批量的 1.845 倍）。关的是**升级动作**，
    不是 ``fallback_policy`` 本身——那个字段是整套批量传输编排（提交等待、节流、24 小时
    截止）的开关，摘掉它 2026-09-15 实测会让批量退化成一次普通 HTTP 直连并挂到客户端超时。
    所以这里走的是既有的 ``online_allowed`` 那条路：拒绝表达为 ``exhausted``，素材按普通失败
    收尾进死信，而不是 ``held``（那会每 15 分钟重试一次、永远占着在飞名额）。

    默认 ``1``（开），不设值的部署行为不变。
    """
    raw = (os.environ if environ is None else environ).get(ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, '1').strip()
    if raw not in {'0', '1'}:
        raise ValueError(f'{ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV} must be 0 or 1')
    return raw == '1'


def transport_decision(rows, *, prefix, queued_at, now, online_limit, batch_only=False,
                      online_allowed=True):
    # 部署开关与按 run 冻结的资格是与的关系：谁说不转，就不转。``decide`` 本身保持纯函数。
    online_allowed = online_allowed and online_escalation_enabled()
    exempt = [row for row in rows if row.status == 'rejected' or is_resource_rejection(row)
              or (batch_only and (is_operator_interruption(row) or is_wait_cap_timeout(row)))]
    exempt_ids = {id(row) for row in exempt}
    counted = [row for row in rows if id(row) not in exempt_ids]
    decision = decide(queued_at=now if batch_only else queued_at, now=now, online_limit=online_limit,
        online_allowed=online_allowed,
        batch_count=sum(row.invocation_key.startswith(prefix+'batch:') for row in counted),
        online_count=sum(row.invocation_key.startswith(prefix+'online:') for row in counted),
        online_started=any(row.status != 'rejected' and row.invocation_key.startswith(prefix+'online:') for row in rows))
    if batch_only and decision.mode != 'batch':
        # No fourth batch evaluation, no online dispatch, no reversal of
        # already-started online history. Operator review can release the hold.
        decision = replace(decision, mode='held', attempt_no=None)
    # Logical evaluation number may stay unchanged. Every physical request must
    # still acquire a fresh ledger identity (including across runtime restarts).
    return replace(decision, reservation_no=1+len(exempt))


OVERLOAD_RETRY_SECONDS = 60


def resource_retry_at(rows, *, prefix, mode):
    """Stable persisted backoff after a confirmed 429, honouring a longer Retry-After.

    RequestBurstTooFast (and any 429 without a known provider code) keeps the
    1/2/4/8/15 minute ladder with jitter. ServerOverloaded waits a flat minute
    with the same jitter: the rejection itself already occupied the slot for
    ~5.5 minutes on the provider's entry queue, it costs nothing, and it says
    nothing about the provider's load at retry time (2026-09-12: 16-40% at
    both 2.4 and 35-51 RPM). Escalating to 15 minutes per streak only idled an
    in-flight material (2026-09-17: 841 overloads, 22 h of ladder waits in 7 h).
    """
    relevant = sorted((row for row in rows if row.status != 'rejected'
        and row.invocation_key.startswith(prefix+mode+':')), key=lambda row: row.id)
    streak = []
    for row in reversed(relevant):
        if not is_resource_rejection(row):
            break
        streak.append(row)
    if not streak:
        return None
    latest = streak[0]
    raw = json.loads(latest.evidence_json or '{}')
    failure = raw.get('failure', {}) if isinstance(raw, Mapping) else {}
    retry_after = failure.get('retry_after_seconds', 0) if isinstance(failure, Mapping) else 0
    if type(retry_after) not in {int, float} or not 0 <= retry_after <= 86400:
        retry_after = 0
    jitter_key = f'{latest.id}:{latest.invocation_key}'
    jitter = int(hashlib.sha256(jitter_key.encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16) / 0xffffffff
    if failure.get('provider_error_code') == 'ServerOverloaded':
        delay = max(retry_after, OVERLOAD_RETRY_SECONDS * (1+jitter*.1))
    else:
        delay = max(retry_after, min(900, 60 * 2**min(len(streak)-1, 4) * (1+jitter*.1)))
    return utc(latest.finished_at or latest.created_at) + timedelta(seconds=delay)


def invocation_prefix(invocation_key):
    # Foreach retry changes attempt suffix, not the identity of the child.
    identity = re.sub(r':attempt:\d+$', '', invocation_key)
    return 'transport-v1:'+hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()+':'


def policy(runtime):
    route = runtime.get('inference_route')
    result = route.get('fallback_policy') if isinstance(route, Mapping) else None
    if result is None:
        return None
    if (route.get('mode') != 'batch' or not isinstance(result, Mapping)
            or result.get('version') != VERSION
            or result.get('batch_max_attempts') != BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS
            or result.get('deadline_seconds') != DEADLINE_SECONDS):
        raise ValueError('invalid frozen batch fallback policy')
    return result


def effective_route(runtime, transport_mode):
    frozen_policy = policy(runtime)
    route = runtime.get('inference_route')
    if transport_mode is None:
        return route
    if frozen_policy is None or transport_mode not in {'batch', 'online'}:
        raise ValueError('transport override not authorized by frozen runtime')
    if transport_mode == 'batch':
        return route
    online = frozen_policy.get('online_route')
    endpoint = online.get('endpoint_id') if isinstance(online, Mapping) else None
    if (not isinstance(online, Mapping) or online.get('mode') != 'online'
            or not isinstance(endpoint, str) or not endpoint.startswith('ep-')
            or endpoint.startswith('ep-bi-')):
        raise ValueError('valid frozen online endpoint required')
    return online

===== FILE backend/app/batch_experiment.py =====
"""Audited, per-material batch-only experiments, outside frozen business facts."""
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document, prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import select

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .effective_batch_clock import restart_batch_window
from .models import AuditEvent, BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun, ProductionRun

HOLD_CODE = 'model_batch_experiment_hold'
ACTION = 'batch_experiment_policy'
COST_GATE_ACTION = 'batch_experiment_cost_gate'


def baseline_identity(baseline):
    """Seal only immutable aggregate facts, excluding progress and metrics."""
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({key: getattr(baseline, key) for key in (
        'id', 'baseline_set_id', 'workflow_version_id', 'total',
        'baseline_set_fingerprint', 'execution_snapshot_json',
        'strategy_snapshot_json', 'correction_contract_hash',
    )}).encode()).hexdigest()


def set_experiment_cost_gate(db, *, baseline_run_id, disabled, expected_identity, incident, actor):
    """Operator-only, reversible grant; no public API or frozen snapshot edits.

    The aggregate lock is shared with the final reservation boundary. No child
    locks are acquired here (reservation lock order is child -> aggregate).
    Only the batch-only materials present in this grant can use the exemption.
    Existing in-flight reservations and every cost row remain untouched.
    """
    if (type(baseline_run_id) is not int or type(disabled) is not bool
            or not isinstance(incident, str) or not 1 <= len(incident) <= 80
            or not isinstance(actor, str) or not actor.strip() or len(actor) > 80):
        raise ValueError('invalid experiment cost gate scope')
    from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    baseline = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.id == baseline_run_id)
        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if baseline is None or baseline_identity(baseline) != expected_identity:
        raise ValueError('experiment baseline identity mismatch')
    key = f'batch-cost-gate:{baseline_run_id}:{incident}:{int(disabled)}'
    previous = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key))
    if previous is not None:
        return previous
    snapshots = {}
    if disabled:
        pairs = db.execute(select(ProductionRun, BaselineRegressionItem).join(
            BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id == ProductionRun.id
        ).where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == baseline_run_id)).all()
        prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, [run for run, _ in pairs])
        for run, item in pairs:
            runtime = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get('runtime_context', {})
            if (run.source_type == 'baseline_regression_item' and run.source_id == item.id
                    and runtime.get('baseline_run_id') == baseline_run_id
                    and runtime.get('inference_route', {}).get('mode') == 'batch'
                    and batch_only_experiment(db, run)):
                snapshots[str(run.id)] = run.snapshot_hash
        if not snapshots:
            raise ValueError('no authorized batch-only experiment materials')
    return append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_budget', action=COST_GATE_ACTION,
        subject_type='baseline_regression_run', subject_id=baseline_run_id, actor=actor, event_key=key,
        payload={'incident': incident, 'baseline_identity': expected_identity,
                 'disabled': disabled, 'production_snapshots': snapshots,
                 'scope': 'cost_reservation_gate_only'})


def experiment_cost_gate_event(db, run, baseline_run_id):
    """Read under the reservation's aggregate lock; stale grants fail closed."""
    event = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(
        AuditEvent.subject_type == 'baseline_regression_run',
        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(baseline_run_id), AuditEvent.action == COST_GATE_ACTION,
    ).order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()).limit(1))
    if event is None:
        return None
    payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    baseline = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, baseline_run_id, populate_existing=True)
    if (baseline is None or payload.get('baseline_identity') != baseline_identity(baseline)
            or type(payload.get('disabled')) is not bool
            or payload.get('scope') != 'cost_reservation_gate_only'
            or not isinstance(payload.get('production_snapshots'), dict)):
        raise ValueError('experiment cost gate identity mismatch')
    if not payload['disabled'] or str(run.id) not in payload['production_snapshots']:
        return None
    if payload['production_snapshots'][str(run.id)] != run.snapshot_hash:
        raise ValueError('experiment material identity mismatch')
    item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id) if run.source_type == 'baseline_regression_item' else None
    if (item is None or item.run_id != baseline_run_id or item.production_run_id != run.id
            or not batch_only_experiment(db, run)):
        return None
    return event


def batch_only_experiment(db, run):
    event = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(
        AuditEvent.subject_type == 'production_run',
        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(run.id), AuditEvent.action == ACTION,
    ).order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()).limit(1))
    if event is None:
        return False
    payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    if payload.get('snapshot_hash') != run.snapshot_hash or type(payload.get('batch_only')) is not bool:
        raise ValueError('batch experiment identity mismatch')
    return payload['batch_only']


def set_batch_experiment(db, *, run_id, baseline_run_id, enabled, incident, actor, now=None):
    """Operator operation; run lock serializes policy changes and reservations.

    Both start and release require a paused, drained material. Refresh is an
    INSERT-only audit event: original timestamps, snapshots and charges stay.
    The caller selects the explicitly approved baseline, never a global flag.
    """
    if type(enabled) is not bool or type(baseline_run_id) is not int:
        raise ValueError('invalid batch experiment scope')
    from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None or run.source_type != 'baseline_regression_item':
        raise ValueError('batch experiment requires a baseline material')
    item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, run.source_id)
    runtime = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get('runtime_context', {})
    if (item is None or item.run_id != baseline_run_id or item.production_run_id != run.id
            or runtime.get('baseline_run_id') != baseline_run_id
            or runtime.get('inference_route', {}).get('mode') != 'batch'):
        raise ValueError('batch experiment baseline or route mismatch')
    if not isinstance(incident, str) or not 1 <= len(incident) <= 80:
        raise ValueError('invalid batch experiment incident')
    key = f'batch-experiment:{run.id}:{incident}:{int(enabled)}'
    previous = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key))
    if previous:
        return previous
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    restart_batch_window(db, run_id=run.id, incident=f'{incident}:{int(enabled)}', actor=actor, now=now)
    return append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_clock', action=ACTION,
        subject_type='production_run', subject_id=run.id, actor=actor, event_key=key,
        payload={'incident': incident, 'baseline_run_id': baseline_run_id,
                 'batch_only': enabled, 'snapshot_hash': run.snapshot_hash},
        created_at=now)

===== FILE backend/app/batch_inference.py =====
"""Explicit batch transport and immutable non-audio price contract."""
from copy import deepcopy
from types import SimpleNamespace

from .model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing, is_volcengine_ark_provider

# Business deadline approved 2026-09-10; expiry preserves unknown billing.
BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 600
# Frozen batch-endpoint provider wait cap (ADR-0084, 2026-09-12). Observed
# maxima: HTTP 200 356.9 s, ServerOverloaded 94.8% within 300-359 s, hung
# calls 80-160 min. 420 s keeps 63 s above the longest observed success and
# only reclaims slots from hangs; never lower than 360 (would kill successes).
BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS = 420
BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_MIN_SECONDS = 360
BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_ENV = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS'


def provider_wait_cap_seconds():
    """Per-request provider wait cap for ep-bi batch calls, or None if disabled.

    The cap applies only to the local wait; a capped call is settled as an
    unknown outcome with its fee liability retained, never as a free cancel.
    """
    import os
    raw = os.getenv(BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_ENV, '')
    if raw.strip().lower() in {'', 'default'}:
        return float(BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS)
    if raw.strip().lower() in {'0', 'off', 'none'}:
        return None
    value = float(raw)
    if not BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_MIN_SECONDS <= value <= 86400:
        raise ValueError(f'{BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_ENV} must be {BATCH_PROVIDER_WAIT_CAP_MIN_SECONDS}..86400 seconds, 0 to disable')
    return value


WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_BASE_SECONDS = 60
WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_FACTOR = 3
WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_MAX_SECONDS = 900


def wait_cap_resubmit_delay(attempt_no):
    """第几次命中本地等待上限，就等多久再按断点重提交：60 → 180 → 540 → 900（封顶）。

    2026-09-16 实测：24 小时内 1,589 次命中上限后的重提交，1,066 次（67%）间隔正好 60 秒——
    原先写死的 60 就是生效值，冻结策略里没有任何退避。同一步骤反复命中上限（平均 2.17 次，
    最多 7 次）说明供应商在过载，60 秒后原样再撞一次只是把负载推回去，是自己给自己加的
    放大器（每素材 1.56 次成功却发出 4.24 次）。

    首次仍是 60 秒——供应商可能只是抖了一下；之后按 3 倍拉开，900 秒封顶与等待上限同量级。
    ``attempt_no`` 是步骤尝试号：命中上限走非持久等待分支，每次都会新开一次尝试，所以它就是
    「第几次命中」的计数。传 None 或非正数按第一次处理。
    """
    try:
        n = int(attempt_no)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        n = 1
    n = max(1, n)
    return float(min(WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_BASE_SECONDS * WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_FACTOR ** (n - 1),
                     WAIT_CAP_RESUBMIT_MAX_SECONDS))


def timeout_retry_delay(*, enabled, api_path, attempt_no, error_type, complete_parent):
    """One opt-in root-node retry, still subject to frozen policy and budget.

    This does not declare the old provider operation canceled or uncharged.
    Foreach/correction and non-timeout unknown outcomes remain excluded.
    """
    if (enabled is True and api_path == '/batch/chat/completions'
            and type(attempt_no) is int and attempt_no == 1
            and error_type == 'timeout' and complete_parent is True):
        return 300
    return None


def batch_pricing_contract(provider, model_id):
    if not is_volcengine_ark_provider(provider) or model_id != 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428':
        raise ValueError('批量推理当前仅验收了 Doubao Seed 2.0 Lite 260428')
    contract = deepcopy(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    contract['effective_at'] = '2026-09-09T11:00:00+08:00'
    for tier, rates in zip(contract['tiers'], ((300,120,1800),(450,180,2700),(900,360,5400))):
        tier['input_micros_per_1k'], tier['cached_input_micros_per_1k'], tier['output_micros_per_1k'] = rates
    return contract


def configure_batch_client(config, endpoint_id):
    if not endpoint_id or not endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-'):
        return
    batch_pricing_contract(config.provider, config.model_id)
    if config.protocol != 'openai_chat':
        raise ValueError('批量接入点需要 Chat Completions 协议')
    config.api_path = '/batch/chat/completions'
    config.timeout_seconds = BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
    config.max_retries = 0


def pricing_models_for_route(models, route):
    if not route or route.get('mode') != 'batch':
        return models
    result = {}
    for key, model in models.items():
        if is_volcengine_ark_provider(model.provider) and model.model_id.startswith('doubao-seed-2-'):
            try:
                contract = batch_pricing_contract(model.provider, model.model_id)
            except ValueError as exc:
                from .workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError('batch_model_unsupported', str(exc), status_code=422) from exc
            values = {k:v for k,v in vars(model).items() if k != '_sa_instance_state'}
            clone = SimpleNamespace(**values)
            import json
            clone.pricing_contract_json = json.dumps(contract)
            if route.get('fallback_policy', {}).get('version') == 'batch-online-fallback-v1':
                clone._online_fallback_model = model
            result[key] = clone
        else:
            result[key] = model
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/batch_job_jsonl.py =====
"""Batch Job wire format; never changes frozen inference/scoring parameters."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit


def custom_id(node_key):
    if not isinstance(node_key, str) or not node_key:
        raise ValueError("node identity required")
    return hashlib.sha256(node_key.encode()).hexdigest()


def request_jsonl(rows, *, max_bytes=32*1024*1024):
    seen = set()
    result = bytearray()
    for node_key, body in rows:
        key = custom_id(node_key)
        if key in seen:
            raise ValueError("duplicate custom_id")
        if not isinstance(body, dict):
            raise ValueError("body must be an object")
        seen.add(key)
        line = json.dumps({"custom_id": key, "body": body}, ensure_ascii=False,
                          allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode()+b"\n"
        if len(result)+len(line) > max_bytes:
            raise ValueError("batch file exceeds configured bound")
        result.extend(line)
    if not seen:
        raise ValueError("empty batch")
    return bytes(result)


def validate_tos_url(url, *, bucket, prefix):
    if not bucket or not prefix or not prefix.endswith("/") or prefix.startswith("/"):
        raise ValueError("explicit bucket and directory prefix required")
    location = urlsplit(url)
    if not location.path.startswith("/"):
        raise ValueError("TOS object path required")
    key = unquote(location.path[1:])
    if (location.username or location.password or location.port or location.fragment
            or any(part in {"..", ".", ""} for part in key.split("/"))
            or not key.startswith(prefix)):
        raise ValueError("TOS object outside approved scope")
    valid = ((location.scheme == "tos" and location.netloc == bucket and not location.query)
             or (location.scheme == "https" and location.netloc == f"{bucket}.tos-cn-beijing.volces.com"))
    if not valid:
        raise ValueError("Beijing TOS URL required")
    return url


def materialize_images(body, *, upload, bucket, prefix):
    """Upload receives the EXACT serialized image bytes, MIME and SHA256.

    The caller supplies a scoped uploader. No credentials are accepted or
    persisted here. Signed URL expiry is a deployment-side contract.
    """
    result = deepcopy(body)
    for message in result.get("messages", []):
        content = message.get("content")
        if not isinstance(content, list):
            continue
        for part in content:
            if part.get("type") != "image_url":
                continue
            image = part.get("image_url")
            if not isinstance(image, dict) or not isinstance(image.get("url"), str):
                raise ValueError("invalid image_url")
            url = image["url"]
            if url.startswith("data:"):
                header, encoded = url.split(",", 1)
                if header not in {"data:image/png;base64", "data:image/jpeg;base64", "data:image/webp;base64"}:
                    raise ValueError("unsupported prepared image MIME")
                if len(encoded) > 48*1024*1024:
                    raise ValueError("prepared image exceeds bound")
                blob = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
                if not blob:
                    raise ValueError("empty prepared image")
                url = upload(blob, header[5:-7], hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest())
            image["url"] = validate_tos_url(url, bucket=bucket, prefix=prefix)
    return result


def _unique_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError("duplicate JSON key")
        result[key] = value
    return result


class ResultStream:
    """One bounded object stream. Keep incomplete tails until the next chunk.

    An active object's missing final newline is not a malformed provider result.
    Only call finish after an object is known complete. Re-reading snapshots is
    safe because persistent receipts, rather than byte offsets, own deduplication.
    """
    def __init__(self, *, max_line_bytes=2*1024*1024):
        if type(max_line_bytes) is not int or max_line_bytes <= 0:
            raise ValueError("positive line bound required")
        self.bound = max_line_bytes
        self.tail = bytearray()
        self.errors = []
        self.error_count = 0
        self.line_number = 1
        self.dropping = False

    def _error(self, reason):
        self.error_count += 1
        if len(self.errors) < 100:
            self.errors.append({"line": self.line_number, "reason": reason})

    def _consume(self):
        if not self.tail.strip():
            return []
        try:
            return [self._decode(bytes(self.tail))]
        except (ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError) as exc:
            self._error(type(exc).__name__)
            return []

    def _decode(self, line):
        value = json.loads(line, object_pairs_hook=_unique_object,
                           parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError("invalid JSON number")))
        if not isinstance(value, dict) or not isinstance(value.get("custom_id"), str) or not value["custom_id"]:
            raise ValueError("result custom_id missing")
        if not isinstance(value.get("response"), dict) and not isinstance(value.get("error"), dict):
            raise ValueError("result response/error missing")
        return value

    def feed(self, chunk):
        if not isinstance(chunk, bytes):
            raise ValueError("result chunk must be bytes")
        results = []
        start = 0
        while start < len(chunk):
            newline = chunk.find(b"\n", start)
            end = len(chunk) if newline == -1 else newline
            if not self.dropping and len(self.tail)+end-start > self.bound:
                self._error("line_too_large")
                self.tail.clear()
                self.dropping = True
            if not self.dropping:
                self.tail.extend(chunk[start:end])
            if newline == -1:
                break
            if not self.dropping:
                results.extend(self._consume())
            self.tail.clear()
            self.dropping = False
            self.line_number += 1
            start = newline+1
        return results

    def finish(self):
        result = self._consume() if not self.dropping else []
        self.tail.clear()
        self.dropping = False
        return result

===== FILE backend/app/batch_job_runtime.py =====
"""Durable coordination for detached model jobs.

No provider I/O or sleep occurs in these transactions. The caller commits a
submission ticket BEFORE making a paid request. Unknown submissions stay owned;
neither a poll failure nor a local lease expiry authorizes another paid call.

This store is deliberately not registered in production startup until the
provider adapter and workflow bridge have passed their integration gates.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import (Column, Integer, MetaData, String, Table, Text,
                        insert, select, update, func)


metadata = MetaData()
nodes = Table("batch_job_nodes", metadata,
    Column("node_key", String(240), primary_key=True),
    Column("group_key", String(240), nullable=False, index=True),
    Column("request_digest", String(64), nullable=False),
    Column("queued_us", String(20), nullable=False, index=True),
    Column("batch_key", String(64), nullable=True, index=True),
    Column("online_key", String(64), nullable=True, unique=True),
    Column("online_state", String(20), nullable=False, default="none"),
    Column("winner", String(20), nullable=True),
    Column("result_json", Text, nullable=True),
    Column("delivered", Integer, nullable=False, default=0),
)
jobs = Table("batch_job_submissions", metadata,
    Column("job_key", String(64), primary_key=True),
    Column("group_key", String(240), nullable=False),
    Column("remote_id", String(240), nullable=True, unique=True),
    Column("status", String(30), nullable=False),
)
result_receipts = Table("batch_job_receipts", metadata,
    Column("receipt_key", String(64), primary_key=True),
    Column("node_key", String(240), nullable=False, index=True),
    Column("transport", String(20), nullable=False),
    Column("call_key", String(64), nullable=False),
    Column("digest", String(64), nullable=False),
    Column("result_json", Text, nullable=False),
    Column("usage_json", Text, nullable=False),
)
locks = Table("batch_job_coordinator_lock", metadata,
    Column("id", Integer, primary_key=True),
    Column("revision", Integer, nullable=False),
)


def initialize(connection):
    """Deployment/migration initialization, never during a polling tick."""
    metadata.create_all(connection)
    if connection.execute(select(locks.c.id).where(locks.c.id == 1)).first() is None:
        connection.execute(insert(locks).values(id=1, revision=0))


def _lock(db):
    # A write serializes SQLite as well as MySQL. All coordinator writes take
    # this first, so count+claim and result-vs-fallback decisions are atomic.
    count = db.execute(update(locks).where(locks.c.id == 1)
                       .values(revision=locks.c.revision+1)).rowcount
    if count != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("batch job store not initialized")


def _json(value):
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                      separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)


def _us(now):
    if not isinstance(now, datetime) or now.tzinfo is None:
        raise ValueError("timezone-aware enqueue time required")
    epoch = datetime(1970, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    delta = now.astimezone(timezone.utc)-epoch
    value = (delta.days*86400+delta.seconds)*1_000_000+delta.microseconds
    if value < 0:
        raise ValueError("enqueue time must be after epoch")
    return str(value).zfill(20)


def _text(value, size):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > size:
        raise ValueError("invalid identity")
    return value


def get_node(db, node_key):
    row = db.execute(select(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key == node_key)).mappings().first()
    return dict(row) if row else None


def enqueue(db, *, node_key, group_key, request_digest, queued_at):
    _text(node_key, 240)
    _text(group_key, 240)
    if len(request_digest) != 64 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in request_digest):
        raise ValueError("invalid request digest")
    queued_us = _us(queued_at)
    _lock(db)
    existing = get_node(db, node_key)
    if existing:
        if (existing["group_key"], existing["request_digest"], existing["queued_us"]) != (
                group_key, request_digest, queued_us):
            raise ValueError("node identity conflicts with frozen request")
        return existing
    db.execute(insert(nodes).values(node_key=node_key, group_key=group_key,
        request_digest=request_digest, queued_us=queued_us, online_state="none", delivered=0))
    return get_node(db, node_key)


def claim_batch(db, *, group_key, limit, now, max_active_jobs=20):
    if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= 10000:
        raise ValueError("batch size must be 1..10000")
    if type(max_active_jobs) is not int or not 1 <= max_active_jobs <= 20:
        raise ValueError("active job limit must be 1..20")
    _lock(db)
    active = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(jobs).where(
        jobs.c.status.in_(["submitting", "running"])))
    if active >= max_active_jobs:
        return None
    cutoff = str(int(_us(now))-86_400_000_000).zfill(20)
    keys = list(db.scalars(select(nodes.c.node_key).where(
        nodes.c.group_key == group_key, nodes.c.batch_key.is_(None),
        nodes.c.online_key.is_(None), nodes.c.winner.is_(None),
        nodes.c.queued_us > cutoff,
    ).order_by(nodes.c.queued_us, nodes.c.node_key).limit(limit)))
    if not keys:
        return None
    key = uuid4().hex
    db.execute(insert(jobs).values(job_key=key, group_key=group_key, status="submitting"))
    db.execute(update(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key.in_(keys)).values(batch_key=key))
    return key


def confirm_submission(db, job_key, remote_id):
    _text(remote_id, 240)
    _lock(db)
    job = db.execute(select(jobs).where(jobs.c.job_key == job_key)).mappings().first()
    if not job or (job["remote_id"] is not None and job["remote_id"] != remote_id):
        raise ValueError("submission identity conflict")
    if job["remote_id"] == remote_id:
        return
    db.execute(update(jobs).where(jobs.c.job_key == job_key).values(
        remote_id=remote_id, status="running"))


def pending_jobs(db):
    return [dict(r) for r in db.execute(select(jobs).where(
        jobs.c.status.in_(["submitting", "running"]))).mappings()]


def finish_job(db, *, job_key, remote_id, phase):
    """Release admission only on a provider-confirmed terminal phase.

    Missing rows keep their original deadlines/unknown fees; terminal job state
    never fabricates success or authorizes another batch submission.
    """
    if phase not in {"Completed", "Terminated", "Failed"}:
        raise ValueError("provider terminal phase required")
    _lock(db)
    changed = db.execute(update(jobs).where(jobs.c.job_key == job_key,
        jobs.c.remote_id == remote_id, jobs.c.status == "running")
        .values(status=phase.lower())).rowcount
    if not changed:
        prior = db.execute(select(jobs).where(jobs.c.job_key == job_key)).mappings().first()
        if not prior or prior["remote_id"] != remote_id or prior["status"] != phase.lower():
            raise ValueError("terminal job identity conflict")


def claim_online(db, *, limit, now):
    """Limit is the global number of online tickets, including unknown calls.

    Tickets are not automatically expired/reclaimed: a crashed request may
    still incur cost. A provider-confirmed outcome releases its active slot.
    """
    if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= 24:
        raise ValueError("online limit must be 1..24")
    _lock(db)
    active = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(nodes).where(
        nodes.c.online_state == "inflight"))
    capacity = max(0, limit-active)
    if not capacity:
        return []
    cutoff = str(int(_us(now))-86_400_000_000).zfill(20)
    rows = db.execute(select(nodes).where(nodes.c.winner.is_(None),
        nodes.c.online_key.is_(None), nodes.c.queued_us <= cutoff,
    ).order_by(nodes.c.queued_us, nodes.c.node_key).limit(capacity)).mappings().all()
    tickets = []
    for row in rows:
        key = uuid4().hex
        db.execute(update(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key == row["node_key"]).values(
            online_key=key, online_state="inflight"))
        tickets.append({"node_key": row["node_key"], "call_key": key,
                        "request_digest": row["request_digest"]})
    return tickets


def record_result(db, *, node_key, transport, call_key, result, usage, now):
    """Accept an already contract-validated result, never an unchecked response.

    Persist every distinct paid call's receipt even when another call won.
    Receipt usage is evidence, NOT proof that provider billing is reconciled.
    """
    if transport not in {"batch", "online"} or not isinstance(result, dict) or not isinstance(usage, dict):
        raise ValueError("invalid result envelope")
    _us(now)
    result_json, usage_json = _json(result), _json(usage)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(_json([result, usage]).encode()).hexdigest()
    receipt_key = hashlib.sha256(_json([node_key, transport, call_key]).encode()).hexdigest()
    _lock(db)
    node = get_node(db, node_key)
    if not node or node[f"{transport}_key"] != call_key:
        raise ValueError("result call does not belong to node")
    prior = db.execute(select(result_receipts).where(
        result_receipts.c.receipt_key == receipt_key)).mappings().first()
    if prior:
        if prior["digest"] != digest:
            raise ValueError("conflicting duplicate result")
        return False
    db.execute(insert(result_receipts).values(receipt_key=receipt_key, node_key=node_key,
        transport=transport, call_key=call_key, digest=digest,
        result_json=result_json, usage_json=usage_json))
    if transport == "online":
        db.execute(update(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key == node_key).values(online_state="completed"))
    won = db.execute(update(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key == node_key,
        nodes.c.winner.is_(None)).values(winner=transport, result_json=result_json)).rowcount
    return won == 1


def ready_results(db, *, limit=100):
    if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= 10000:
        raise ValueError("result limit must be 1..10000")
    rows = db.execute(select(nodes).where(nodes.c.winner.is_not(None),
        nodes.c.delivered == 0).order_by(nodes.c.node_key).limit(limit)).mappings()
    return [{"node_key": r["node_key"], "winner": r["winner"],
             "result": json.loads(r["result_json"])} for r in rows]


def ack_result(db, node_key, *, winner):
    """Call in the SAME transaction as workflow continuation/outbox insertion."""
    if winner not in {"batch", "online"}:
        raise ValueError("valid winner required")
    _lock(db)
    count = db.execute(update(nodes).where(nodes.c.node_key == node_key,
        nodes.c.winner == winner).values(delivered=1)).rowcount
    if count != 1:
        raise ValueError("result acknowledgement identity conflict")


def receipts(db):
    return [dict(r) for r in db.execute(select(result_receipts)).mappings()]

===== FILE backend/app/batch_job_workflow.py =====
"""Transaction bridge; cloud activation is gated outside this module."""
from datetime import timedelta
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import select

from . import batch_job_runtime as batch
from .models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, utcnow
from .workflow_runtime import (
    RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES, WorkflowRuntimeError, _close_dispatch, complete_runtime_step,
)


def node_key(attempt_id):
    return f"production-step:{attempt_id}:root"


def _locked_step(db, attempt_id):
    initial = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    if initial is None:
        raise ValueError("step missing")
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id == initial.run_id)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == attempt_id)
                        .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    return run, attempt


def detach(db, *, attempt_id, lease_token, group_key, request_digest, material_queued_at):
    """Queue request and retire executor lease together, before provider I/O."""
    batch._lock(db)
    run, attempt = _locked_step(db, attempt_id)
    if (run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES or attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
            or not lease_token or attempt.lease_token != lease_token):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("step_lease_stale", "批量任务准备租约已失效", status_code=409)
    batch.enqueue(db, node_key=node_key(attempt_id), group_key=group_key,
                  request_digest=request_digest, queued_at=material_queued_at)
    dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
        RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt_id).with_for_update())
    now = utcnow()
    if dispatch is not None:
        _close_dispatch(dispatch, status="completed", error_code="batch_job_wait",
                        error_message="等待批量任务逐条结果", now=now)
    attempt.status = "retryable"
    attempt.last_error_code = "batch_job_wait"
    attempt.last_error_message = "等待批量结果；满24小时进入在线兜底队列"
    attempt.lease_owner = attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = attempt.heartbeat_at = None
    run.lease_owner = run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    db.flush()


def resume(db, *, attempt_id):
    """Finalize one validated node and acknowledge in a single DB transaction.

    Existing workflow completion owns frozen short circuits, successor dispatch,
    final result generation and downstream correction precedence. No batch-wide
    barrier or placeholder downstream write is introduced.
    """
    batch._lock(db)
    key = node_key(attempt_id)
    item = batch.get_node(db, key)
    if not item or not item["winner"] or item["delivered"]:
        return False
    run, attempt = _locked_step(db, attempt_id)
    if run.status in {"canceled", "failed", "succeeded"}:
        batch.ack_result(db, key, winner=item["winner"])
        return False
    if run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        return False  # paused/blocked: preserve the result for explicit resume
    if attempt.status != "retryable" or attempt.last_error_code != "batch_job_wait":
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("batch_job_resume_conflict", "批量结果对应步骤状态已改变", status_code=409)
    import json
    token = uuid4().hex
    attempt.status = "running"
    attempt.lease_owner = "batch-job-result"
    attempt.lease_token = token
    attempt.lease_expires_at = utcnow()+timedelta(minutes=5)
    db.flush()
    complete_runtime_step(db, attempt_id, token, json.loads(item["result_json"]))
    batch.ack_result(db, key, winner=item["winner"])
    return True

===== FILE backend/app/batch_pacing_maintenance.py =====
"""Explicit pool maintenance intervals, serialized with physical admissions.

No inferred downtime, no invocation edits, no process-local pacing state.
One incident opens on its first successful child drain and closes on its first
resume. This deliberately undercounts a staggered drain/resume's tail.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import select
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .models import AuditEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation


def merge_intervals(intervals, start, end):
    merged = []
    for left, right in sorted(intervals):
        left, right = max(start, left), min(end, right)
        if right <= left:
            continue
        if merged and left <= merged[-1][1]:
            merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], max(merged[-1][1], right))
        else:
            merged.append((left, right))
    return merged


def record_pool_maintenance(db, *, pool_id, incident, paused, actor, now=None):
    from .model_quota import _locked_pool, reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
    if not isinstance(incident, str) or not 1 <= len(incident) <= 180:
        raise ValueError('invalid pacing maintenance incident')
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    _locked_pool(db, pool_id)
    key = f'batch-pacing:pool:{pool_id}:{incident}'
    events = {r.action: r for r in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(
        AuditEvent.event_key.in_([key+':pause', key+':resume'])))}
    action = 'pacing_maintenance_pause' if paused else 'pacing_maintenance_resume'
    if action in events:
        return events[action]
    if not paused and 'pacing_maintenance_pause' not in events:
        return None  # Historical pre-r7 drain: no invented start time.
    return append_audit_event(db, category='runtime_clock', action=action,
        subject_type='model_quota_pool', subject_id=str(pool_id), actor=actor,
        event_key=key+(':pause' if paused else ':resume'),
        payload={'incident': incident, 'scope': 'batch_submission'},
        created_at=now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))


def record_release_pool_maintenance(db, *, run_id, incident, paused, actor):
    # A warm state necessarily has a physical invocation. Lock in pool-id
    # order after the child lock, matching the run -> pool reservation order.
    pools = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run_id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.like('ep-bi-%'),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id.is_not(None)).distinct().order_by(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id)).all()
    for pool_id in pools:
        record_pool_maintenance(db, pool_id=pool_id, incident=incident,
            paused=paused, actor=actor)


def maintenance_intervals(db, *, pool_id, start, now):
    """Caller holds pool lock; READ COMMITTED sees latest committed events.

    Only closed pairs count: an abandoned/open pause cannot exempt ordinary
    idle forever. The wall-clock 24h limit in the caller is unconditional.
    """
    end = now.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp() if now.tzinfo is None else now.timestamp()
    rows = db.execute(select(AuditEvent.action, AuditEvent.event_key,
        AuditEvent.created_at).where(AuditEvent.subject_type == 'model_quota_pool',
        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(pool_id),
        AuditEvent.action.in_(['pacing_maintenance_pause', 'pacing_maintenance_resume']),
        AuditEvent.created_at >= datetime.fromtimestamp(end-86400, timezone.utc),
        AuditEvent.created_at <= now).order_by(AuditEvent.created_at, AuditEvent.id)).all()
    opened, intervals = {}, []
    for row in rows:
        key = row.event_key.rsplit(':', 1)[0]
        point = row.created_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp()
        if row.action.endswith('pause'):
            opened.setdefault(key, point)
        elif key in opened:
            intervals.append((opened.pop(key), point))
    return merge_intervals(intervals, start, end)

===== FILE backend/app/batch_submission.py =====
"""Cross-worker batch submission pacing, persisted in invocation evidence.

The caller owns the quota-pool lock and must persist the returned state with
the invocation in that transaction. This controls the admission rate; it
does not infer vendor quotas or actual billed tokens from daily allowances.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import random
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import JSON, cast, select, type_coerce

from .model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from .models import WorkflowModelInvocation

VERSION = 'batch-submission-pacing-v1'
ALGORITHM = 'prepared-token-pacing-v2'
WAIT_CODE = 'model_batch_submission_wait'
RAMP_SECONDS = 180
START_RPM = 2.0
START_TPM = 60000.0
# Software estimate ceiling, NOT an inferred provider quota. Actual configured
# pool limits remain authoritative. UTF-8-byte upper estimates need >4.1M/min
# for the 100k/day mixed A/B target even when billed usage is much smaller.
LOCAL_MAX_TPM = 6000000.0
CONTROL_VERSION = 'window-feedback-v4'
# Scoped-429 share of the last RAMP_SECONDS completions (200+429), reported
# for observation only. Live data on 2026-09-12 showed ServerOverloaded at
# 16-40% both at 2.4 RPM and at 35-51 RPM: it tracks the provider's own load,
# not this controller's rate, so it must not hold the ramp. RequestBurstTooFast
# (the vendor's burst protection) still reduces immediately.
OVERLOAD_MIN_SAMPLE = 5
# Above roughly max_concurrency / average call duration it is the slots, not the
# provider, that bound throughput: every further ramp step buys queue depth and
# nothing else, and leaves a high rate primed to fire the instant a block of
# slots frees - which is the burst RequestBurstTooFast exists to punish. Hold
# the rate there. Never lower it: the provider is refusing nothing, we are
# simply out of local capacity, and cutting the rate would only make the
# recovery slower. 0.85 leaves room to keep climbing while slack is real.
SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING = 0.85
# Rejected candidates all compute the same successor slot; spreading their
# retry inside one gap breaks the thundering herd of identical available_at.
WAIT_JITTER_MAX_SECONDS = 15.0
# Advisory preflight only: with N waiters and R admissions per minute, waking
# every waiter once per gap costs N claim/preflight cycles per slot (observed
# ~5 rejected cycles/s and MySQL >100% CPU with 253 waiters at 2.4 RPM). Spread
# wakeups over ~N*20/R seconds so roughly three waiters compete per slot.
WAIT_SPREAD_PER_WAITER_SECONDS = 20.0
WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS = 600.0
CONTINUATION_WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS = 60.0


def _spread_seconds(waiting_hint, rpm, *, continuation=False):
    if not _number(waiting_hint) or waiting_hint <= 1 or not _number(rpm, positive=True):
        return 0.0
    # Do not hide B/retry work from the shared ready-ticket fairness gate for
    # ten minutes. This bounds only extra jitter, never provider/pacing waits.
    ceiling = CONTINUATION_WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS if continuation else WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS
    return random.uniform(0, min(ceiling, waiting_hint*WAIT_SPREAD_PER_WAITER_SECONDS/rpm))
SCOPED_BATCH_ERRORS = frozenset({'ServerOverloaded', 'RequestBurstTooFast'})
BURST_BACKOFF_CHAIN_SECONDS = (5, 10, 20, 40, 60)


def scoped_backoff_seconds(code, hint, ordinal):
    """Pool-wide wait after the ordinal-th scoped 429 of one 180 s window.

    RequestBurstTooFast, the vendor's own burst protection, keeps the 5/10/20/
    40/60 s chain. ServerOverloaded honours only the vendor's Retry-After (1 s
    in every production sample). Measured 2026-09-17 on 6.9 h of production
    after the 420 s wait cap: 841 overload rejections, each arriving ~5.5 min
    after its submission, kept this gate shut 34% of wall time (89 closures,
    p90 347 s, max 455 s) - every arrival pushed the whole pool's retry_at up
    to 60 s further out. Overload tracks the provider's load, not our rate
    (16-40% at both 2.4 and 35-51 RPM on 2026-09-12), so a rejection that
    finishes now only says the provider had no room 5 min ago; stopping every
    waiter for it produced the observed oscillation: a wave of 429s, a full
    stop, a wave of submissions, a wave of 429s. Spending per rejection is
    zero; the pool's max_concurrency still bounds what is in flight.
    """
    hint = min(86400, hint) if _number(hint) else 0
    chain = BURST_BACKOFF_CHAIN_SECONDS[min(max(ordinal, 1)-1, 4)] if code == 'RequestBurstTooFast' else 0
    return max(hint, chain)


def is_scoped_batch_rejection(row, evidence):
    return (row.provider == 'doubao' and (row.endpoint_id or '').startswith('ep-bi-')
            and evidence.get('provider_error_code') in SCOPED_BATCH_ERRORS)


def _feedback(db, pool, now):
    from .runtime_observability import observe_batch_feedback
    with observe_batch_feedback():
        return _feedback_query(db, pool, now)


def _feedback_query(db, pool, now):
    """Recent completions, not submission order; late responses retain scope."""
    rows = db.execute(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json, WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id == pool.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.provider == 'doubao',
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.like('ep-bi-%'),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at > now-timedelta(seconds=RAMP_SECONDS),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at <= now,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code.in_([200, 429]),
    ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at.desc()).limit(4096)).all()
    rejected, burst, retry_at = [], False, 0.0
    for row in reversed(rows):
        if row.upstream_status_code != 429:
            continue
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json or '{}').get('failure', {})
        if evidence.get('provider_error_code') not in SCOPED_BATCH_ERRORS:
            continue  # Unknown errors still use the conservative shared pool cooldown.
        rejected.append(_timestamp(row.finished_at))
        burst |= evidence.get('provider_error_code') == 'RequestBurstTooFast'
        retry_at = max(retry_at, _timestamp(row.finished_at)+scoped_backoff_seconds(
            evidence.get('provider_error_code'), evidence.get('retry_after_seconds', 0), len(rejected)))
    overload_rate = len(rejected)/len(rows) if len(rows) >= OVERLOAD_MIN_SAMPLE else 0.0
    # Only the vendor's own burst protection holds the ramp, which is what the
    # block above OVERLOAD_MIN_SAMPLE always said and what Volcengine confirmed
    # on 2026-09-15: RequestBurstTooFast is the signal to slow down for.
    #
    # A majority rule over this window cannot tell provider load from our own
    # rate. A rejection finishes the instant the provider refuses it; a batch
    # success finishes minutes later. The slower we submit, the more the window
    # fills with rejections whatever the provider is doing, so the rule trips,
    # cuts the rate by a fifth, restarts the ramp, and makes the next trip
    # easier. Production locked at 2 RPM this way - seven ServerOverloaded
    # against five successes, 2.30 -> 1.84 -> 2.21, never climbing - on the pool
    # that had sustained 2,709 RPM on 2026-09-11.
    #
    # Overload no longer feeds the shared retry_at chain either (see
    # scoped_backoff_seconds): only its vendor Retry-After is honoured. A
    # provider_rate_limited pool cooldown still halves the rate.
    pressure = burst
    # A vendor hint can outlive the statistics window. Read only older 429s
    # with long hints; ordinary historical outcomes are not repeatedly loaded.
    # retry_after_hint 是 evidence_json.failure.retry_after_seconds 的生成列（迁移 121），
    # 带索引：这条查询在池锁内执行，原先逐行解析 JSON 扫全部 24 小时 429（正式服 3,770 行、91 ms）。
    older = db.execute(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id == pool.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.provider == 'doubao',
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.like('ep-bi-%'),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code == 429,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.retry_after_hint > RAMP_SECONDS,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at > now-timedelta(seconds=86400),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at <= now-timedelta(seconds=RAMP_SECONDS),
    )).all()
    for row in older:
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json or '{}').get('failure', {})
        hint = evidence.get('retry_after_seconds')
        if evidence.get('provider_error_code') in SCOPED_BATCH_ERRORS and _number(hint):
            retry_at = max(retry_at, _timestamp(row.finished_at)+min(86400, hint))
    return {'completed': len(rows), 'resource_rejections': len(rejected),
            'pressure': pressure, 'overload_rate': round(overload_rate, 4),
            'first_rejection_at': min(rejected, default=0.0),
            'retry_at': retry_at}


def _timestamp(value):
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return (value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)).timestamp()
    return 0.0


def _resource_wait_seconds(db, pool, last_time, now, extra_intervals=()):
    """Count only proven backoff intervals, never ordinary idle or queue age.

    Called only for a would-be cold restart. The finite completion lookback
    reconstructs the same rolling waits as the live controller (burst chain
    and vendor Retry-After; overload contributes only its Retry-After).
    Truncation undercounts waits and therefore remains conservative.
    """
    current = _timestamp(now)
    cutoff = max(last_time-RAMP_SECONDS, current-86400)
    rows = db.execute(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id == pool.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.provider == 'doubao',
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.like('ep-bi-%'),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code == 429,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at > datetime.fromtimestamp(cutoff, timezone.utc),
        WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at <= now,
    ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.finished_at.desc()).limit(4096)).all()
    rejected, intervals = [], []
    for row in reversed(rows):
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json or '{}').get('failure', {})
        if evidence.get('provider_error_code') not in SCOPED_BATCH_ERRORS:
            continue
        point = _timestamp(row.finished_at)
        rejected = [value for value in rejected if value > point-RAMP_SECONDS]
        rejected.append(point)
        delay = scoped_backoff_seconds(evidence.get('provider_error_code'),
                                       evidence.get('retry_after_seconds', 0), len(rejected))
        if delay <= 0:
            continue
        intervals.append((max(last_time, point), min(current, point+delay)))
    from .batch_pacing_maintenance import merge_intervals
    return sum(right-left for left, right in merge_intervals(
        [*intervals, *extra_intervals], last_time, current))


def _number(value, *, positive=False):
    return (type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value)
            and (value > 0 if positive else value >= 0))


def estimate_prepared_submission(prepared):
    """Conservative traffic estimate, never a financial reservation or bill.

    UTF-8 bytes bound the text estimate, including the actual JSON schema and
    image labels. Image payload bytes are transport, not text tokens. Reuse
    the frozen vision estimator for every *sent* image; legacy/unknown media
    fall back to the financial bound instead of silently counting as zero.
    No prompt, URL or base64 is persisted in this evidence.
    """
    request = prepared.serialized_provider_request
    if request is None:
        return None
    media = {f'{m.role}:{m.ordinal}': m for m in prepared.media_bundle.items}
    image_tokens = 0
    for identity in prepared.media_dispatch_identities:
        item = media.get(identity)
        if item is None or type(item.image_tokens) is not int or item.image_tokens <= 0:
            return None
        image_tokens += item.image_tokens
    image_count = 0

    def strip_images(value):
        nonlocal image_count
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            if value.get('type') == 'image_url':
                image_count += 1
                return {'type': 'image_url'}
            return {k: strip_images(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [strip_images(v) for v in value]
        return value

    try:
        body = strip_images(json.loads(request.body))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None
    if image_count != len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities):
        return None
    text_bytes = len(json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf-8'))
    return {'basis': 'prepared-request-v1',
        'tokens': text_bytes + image_tokens + prepared.output_budget,
        'text_utf8_bytes': text_bytes, 'image_tokens': image_tokens,
        'image_count': image_count, 'output_tokens': prepared.output_budget}


def check_submission(db, *, pool, reserved_tokens, now, submission_estimate=None, waiting_hint=None,
                     fair_ticket=None, active_leases=None, continuation=False,
                     fairness_exempt=False):
    """Check under the pool lock without locking another run's invocation.

Online endpoints bypass this gate. All batch endpoints in the provider-owned
pool share it, so separate workflows and processes cannot multiply the ramp.
Only new physical reservations call this; existing identities are replayed
by the budget validator without spending another pacing unit.
"""
    current = _timestamp(now)
    if submission_estimate is not None and (
            not isinstance(submission_estimate, dict)
            or submission_estimate.get('basis') != 'prepared-request-v1'
            or type(submission_estimate.get('tokens')) is not int
            or submission_estimate['tokens'] <= 0):
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable('batch_submission_estimate_invalid',
            '批量请求流量估算无效，未发送请求', pool_key=pool.pool_key)
    pacing_tokens = submission_estimate['tokens'] if submission_estimate else reserved_tokens
    previous = db.execute(select(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.evidence_json, WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.reserved_input_tokens,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.reserved_output_tokens,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.status, WorkflowModelInvocation.input_tokens,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.output_tokens,
    ).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.quota_pool_id == pool.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.provider == 'doubao',
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.like('ep-bi-%'),
    ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()).limit(1)).one_or_none()
    ceiling_rpm = float(pool.requests_per_minute or pool.max_concurrency*60)
    ceiling_tpm = float(pool.tokens_per_minute or LOCAL_MAX_TPM)
    rpm, tpm = min(START_RPM, ceiling_rpm), min(START_TPM, ceiling_tpm)
    ramp_started = current
    last_time = None
    last_tokens = reserved_tokens
    cooldown_seen = 0.0
    reduced_at = 0.0
    fairness_state = None
    if previous is not None:
        last_time = _timestamp(previous.created_at)
        last_tokens = previous.reserved_input_tokens+previous.reserved_output_tokens
        try:
            evidence = json.loads(previous.evidence_json or '{}')
            state = evidence.get('reservation', {}).get('batch_submission')
            if state is not None:
                fairness_state = state.get('fairness') if isinstance(state, dict) else None
                if (not isinstance(state, dict) or state.get('version') != VERSION
                        or any(not _number(state.get(key), positive=True) for key in (
                            'requests_per_minute', 'tokens_per_minute', 'last_admitted_at', 'ramp_started_at'))
                        or not _number(state.get('cooldown_seen_at'))):
                    raise ValueError('invalid pacing evidence')
                rpm = min(state['requests_per_minute'], ceiling_rpm)
                tpm = min(state['tokens_per_minute'], ceiling_tpm)
                last_time = state['last_admitted_at']
                ramp_started = state['ramp_started_at']
                cooldown_seen = state['cooldown_seen_at']
                control = state.get('control_version')
                if control is not None and not isinstance(control, str):
                    raise ValueError('invalid batch controller')
                if control == CONTROL_VERSION:
                    reduced_at = state.get('reduced_at', 0.0)
                    if not _number(reduced_at):
                        raise ValueError('invalid reduction window')
                else:
                    # Explicit controller upgrade starts a fresh *paced*
                    # warmup, never releases a batch of accumulated credits.
                    rpm, tpm = min(START_RPM, ceiling_rpm), min(START_TPM, ceiling_tpm)
                    ramp_started = current
                if state.get('algorithm_version') == ALGORITHM:
                    if not _number(state.get('pacing_tokens')):
                        raise ValueError('invalid pacing token evidence')
                    last_tokens = state['pacing_tokens']
                elif state.get('algorithm_version') is not None:
                    raise ValueError('unknown pacing algorithm')
                elif (previous.status == 'succeeded'
                        and _number(previous.input_tokens, positive=True)
                        and _number(previous.output_tokens)):
                    # One-time upgrade: settled usage is known. An unknown
                    # previous call retains its old upper-bound spacing.
                    last_tokens = previous.input_tokens + previous.output_tokens
        except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ModelQuotaUnavailable('batch_submission_evidence_invalid',
                '批量提交节奏记录损坏，已停止发送并等待核查', pool_key=pool.pool_key) from exc

    cooldown = _timestamp(pool.cooldown_until)
    feedback = _feedback(db, pool, now)
    if feedback['retry_at'] > current:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(WAIT_CODE, '批量资源等待：共享退避后逐步恢复提交',
            pool_key=pool.pool_key, retry_after_seconds=math.ceil(
                feedback['retry_at']-current+_spread_seconds(waiting_hint, rpm, continuation=continuation)))
    new_cooldown = pool.cooldown_reason == 'provider_rate_limited' and cooldown > cooldown_seen
    pressure_at = cooldown if new_cooldown else feedback['first_rejection_at']
    if (new_cooldown or feedback['pressure']) and pressure_at-reduced_at >= RAMP_SECONDS:
        # One aggregate decision per window, not one multiplicative cut for
        # every late response. A bounded probe floor prevents minutes-per-call
        # collapse; provider Retry-After and the shared quota remain binding.
        factor = .5 if new_cooldown else .8
        rpm, tpm = min(ceiling_rpm, max(1.0, rpm*factor)), min(ceiling_tpm, max(30000.0, tpm*factor))
        # Rejected checks do not persist state. Anchor to the fixed cooldown
        # deadline so repeatedly checking cannot postpone recovery forever.
        ramp_started = pressure_at
        reduced_at = pressure_at
    if new_cooldown:
        cooldown_seen = cooldown
    # Counted by the caller inside this same pool lock, so it is the snapshot the
    # concurrency check just admitted against. It excludes this call's own lease,
    # which is acquired after this returns.
    slots = pool.max_concurrency if _number(pool.max_concurrency, positive=True) else 0
    utilisation = active_leases/slots if slots and _number(active_leases) and active_leases >= 0 else None
    saturated = utilisation is not None and utilisation >= SLOT_UTILISATION_CEILING
    idle_threshold = max(RAMP_SECONDS, 2*max(60/rpm, last_tokens*60/tpm))
